\begin{array}{l} \theta_ {t} (y _ {t}) ^ {2} d ^ {2} \Big (\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \Big) ^ {2} \log^ {2} T + \varepsilon_ {\mathrm {s c o r e}, t} (y _ {t}) \sqrt {\theta_ {t} (y _ {t}) d \log T} \Big (\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \Big) + \theta_ {t} (y _ {t}) ^ {3} d ^ {6} \Big (\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \Big) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T + (1 - \alpha_ {t}) d \varepsilon_ {\mathrm {J a c o b i}, t} (y _ {t}) \\ \lesssim \frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T ^ {2}} + \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {3}} + \frac {\varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e} , t} (y _ {t}) \sqrt {d \log^ {3} T}}{T} + \frac {d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i} , t} (y _ {t}) \log T}{T} = o (1), \\ \end{array}
$$

and

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \left(\left\| \int \mathbb {E} \left[ X _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \mid X _ {t} = y _ {t} \right] \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} - \int \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| X _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \mid X _ {t} = y _ {t} \right]\right)}{\left(\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right) \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right)} \right| \\ \leq \left| \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \int \mathbb {E} [ \| X _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} | X _ {t} = y _ {t} ]}{(\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \right| \lesssim \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) d \log T}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \lesssim \frac {d \log^ {2} T}{T} = o (1). \\ \end{array}
$$

With these bounds in mind, applying relations (58a) and (58b) in Lemma 4 leads to

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {p _ {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} Y _ {t - 1}} \left(\phi_ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)\right)}{p _ {Y _ {t}} \left(y _ {t}\right)} \left(\frac {p _ {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} X _ {t - 1}} \left(\phi_ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)\right)}{p _ {X _ {t}} \left(y _ {t}\right)}\right) ^ {- 1} = \frac {p _ {\phi_ {t} \left(Y _ {t}\right)} \left(\phi_ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)\right)}{p _ {Y _ {t}} \left(y _ {t}\right)} \left(\frac {p _ {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} X _ {t - 1}} \left(\phi_ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)\right)}{p _ {X _ {t}} \left(y _ {t}\right)}\right)^ {- 1} \\ = 1 + O \left(\frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T ^ {2}} + \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {3}} + \frac {\varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e} , t} \left(y _ {t}\right) \sqrt {d \log^ {3} T}}{T} + \frac {d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i} , t} \left(y _ {t}\right) \log T}{T}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

for all  $t < \tau(y_T)$ . Using the fact that  $y_{t-1} = \frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}} \phi_t(y_t)$  and invoking the relation (55), we arrive at

$$
\frac {p _ {t - 1} (y _ {t - 1})}{q _ {t - 1} (y _ {t - 1})} = \left\{1 + O \left(\frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T ^ {2}} + \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {3}} + \frac {\varepsilon_ {\mathrm {s c o r e} , t} (y _ {t}) \sqrt {d \log^ {3} T}}{T} + \frac {d \varepsilon_ {\mathrm {J a c o b i} , t} (y _ {t}) \log T}{T}\right) \right\} \frac {p _ {t} (y _ {t})}{q _ {t} (y _ {t})}
$$

for any  $t < \tau(y_T)$ . By abbreviating  $\tau = \tau(y_T)$  for notational simplicity, we reach

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {q _ {1} \left(y _ {1}\right)}{p _ {1} \left(y _ {1}\right)} = \left\{1 + O \left(\frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T} + \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {2}} + S _ {\tau - 1} \left(y _ {\tau - 1}\right)\right) \right\} \frac {q _ {\tau - 1} \left(y _ {\tau - 1}\right)}{p _ {\tau - 1} \left(y _ {\tau - 1}\right)} \\ \in \left[ \frac {p _ {\tau - 1} \left(y _ {\tau - 1}\right)}{2 q _ {\tau - 1} \left(y _ {\tau - 1}\right)}, \frac {2 p _ {\tau - 1} \left(y _ {\tau - 1}\right)}{q _ {\tau - 1} \left(y _ {\tau - 1}\right)} \right], \tag {146a} \\ \end{array}
$$

and similarly,

$$
\frac {q _ {k} \left(y _ {k}\right)}{2 p _ {k} \left(y _ {k}\right)} \leq \frac {q _ {1} \left(y _ {1}\right)}{p _ {1} \left(y _ {1}\right)} \leq 2 \frac {q _ {k} \left(y _ {k}\right)}{p _ {k} \left(y _ {k}\right)}, \quad \forall k <   \tau . \tag {146b}
$$

# B.4 Proof of Lemma 7

In the following, we shall tackle  $\mathcal{I}_2$ ,  $\mathcal{I}_3$  and  $\mathcal{I}_4$  separately. Throughout this proof, we shall abbreviate  $\tau = \tau(Y_T)$  (cf. (67)) whenever it is clear from the context.

The sub-collection in  $\mathcal{I}_2$ . By virtue of the definition (72a) of  $\mathcal{I}_2$ , we make the observation that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {2} \} \right] \stackrel {(i)} {\leq} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {2} \} \frac {S _ {\tau} (Y _ {T})}{c _ {1 4}} \right] \\ \stackrel {\text {(i i)}} {=} \frac {\log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {\tau} _ {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {2} \right\} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t})\right) \right] \\ \stackrel {\text {(i i i)}} {\leq} \frac {2 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {\tau} \mathbb {E} _ {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {2} \right\} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t})\right) \right] \\ = \frac {2 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \sum_ {i \in \mathcal {I} _ {2}, \tau \geq t} \mathbb {E} _ {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {2} \right\} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t})\right) \right] \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \leq \frac {2 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \mathbb {E} _ {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t})\right) \right] \\ = \frac {2 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {t} \sim p _ {t}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t})\right) \right] \\ = \frac {2 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t}) \right] \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\stackrel {\text {(i v)}} {\lesssim} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}} + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}}\right) \log T. \tag {147}
$$

Here, (i) follows since  $S_{\tau}(y_T) \geq c_{14}$  in  $\mathcal{I}_2$  (see (72a)); (ii) comes from the definition of  $S_{t}(\cdot)$  (see (54)); (iii) holds since (by repeating the same proof arguments as for (70) as long as  $2c_{14}$  is small enough)

$$
\frac {p _ {1} \left(y _ {1}\right)}{q _ {1} \left(y _ {1}\right)} \leq \frac {2 p _ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)}{q _ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)}, \quad \forall t \leq \tau ;
$$

and (iv) arises from (49).

The sub-collection in  $\mathcal{I}_3$ . With regards to  $\mathcal{I}_3$  (cf. (72b)), we can derive the following bound in a way similar to (147):

$$
\begin{array}{l} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {3} \right\} \right] \stackrel {{(\mathrm {i})}} {{\leq}} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {3} \right\} \frac {\xi_ {\tau} (Y _ {T})}{c _ {1 4}} \right] \\ = \frac {\log T}{c _ {1 4} T} _ {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} \left(Y _ {1}\right)}{p _ {1} \left(Y _ {1}\right)} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {3} \right\} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, \tau} \left(Y _ {\tau}\right) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, \tau} \left(Y _ {\tau}\right)\right) \right] \\ \stackrel {\mathrm {(i i)}} {\leq} \frac {2 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {\tau - 1} (Y _ {\tau - 1})}{p _ {\tau - 1} (Y _ {\tau - 1})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {3} \right\} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\mathrm {J a c o b i}, \tau} (Y _ {\tau}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\mathrm {s c o r e}, \tau} (Y _ {\tau})\right) \right] \\ = \frac {2 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \mathbb {E} _ {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t - 1} \left(Y _ {t - 1}\right)}{p _ {t - 1} \left(Y _ {t - 1}\right)} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {3} \right\} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} \left(Y _ {t}\right) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} \left(Y _ {t}\right)\right) \mathbb {1} \left\{\tau = t \right\} \right] (148) \\ \stackrel {\mathrm {(i i i)}} {\leq} \frac {1 6 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {\tau} (Y _ {\tau})}{p _ {\tau} (Y _ {\tau})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {3} \right\} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, \tau} (Y _ {\tau}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, \tau} (Y _ {\tau})\right) \right] \\ \leq \frac {1 6 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t})\right) \right] \\ = \frac {1 6 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {t} \sim p _ {t}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})} \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t})\right) \right] \\ = \frac {1 6 \log T}{c _ {1 4} T} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}, t} (Y _ {t}) + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (Y _ {t}) \right] \\ \lesssim \left(d \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}} + \sqrt {d \log T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}}\right) \log T. (149) \\ \end{array}
$$

Here, (i) comes from (72b), (ii) arises from (70b), whereas (iii) is a consequence of (72b).

The sub-collection in  $\mathcal{I}_4$ . We now turn attention to  $\mathcal{I}_4$  (cf. (72c)), towards which we find it helpful to define

$$
\mathcal {J} _ {1, t} := \left\{y _ {T}: \xi_ {t} (y _ {T}) <   c _ {1 4} \right\} \tag {150a}
$$

$$
\mathcal {J} _ {2, t} := \left\{y _ {T}: \xi_ {t} \left(y _ {T}\right) \geq c _ {1 4}, \frac {q _ {t - 1} \left(y _ {t - 1}\right)}{p _ {t - 1} \left(y _ {t - 1}\right)} \leq \frac {8 q _ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)}{p _ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)} \right\} \tag {150b}
$$

$$
\mathcal {J} _ {3, t} := \left\{y _ {T}: \xi_ {t} \left(y _ {T}\right) \geq c _ {1 4}, \frac {q _ {t - 1} \left(y _ {t - 1}\right)}{p _ {t - 1} \left(y _ {t - 1}\right)} > \frac {8 q _ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)}{p _ {t} \left(y _ {t}\right)} \right\} \tag {150c}
$$

for each  $2 \leq t \leq T$ . Equipped with the above definitions, we first make the observation that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {4} \right\} \right] \leq 2 \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {\tau - 1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {\tau - 1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {4} \right\} \right] \\ = 2 \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \mathbb {E} _ {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t - 1} (Y _ {t - 1})}{p _ {t - 1} (Y _ {t - 1})} \mathbb {1} \{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {4} \} \mathbb {1} \{\tau = t \} \right] \\ \leq 2 \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t - 1} \left(Y _ {t - 1}\right)}{p _ {t - 1} \left(Y _ {t - 1}\right)} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {3, t} \right\} \right], \tag {151} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality follows from (70b), and the last line comes from the definition of  $\mathcal{I}_4$  (cf. (72c)) and  $\mathcal{J}_{3,t}$  (cf. (150c)). For notational simplicity, let us define, for  $2\leq t\leq T$

$$
h _ {t} := \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})}.
$$

In view of the second inequality in (150c), one has  $h_{t - 1} > 8h_t$  as long as  $y_{T}\in \mathcal{J}_{3,t}$ . Consequently,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} h _ {t - 1} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {3, t} \right\} \\ <   \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} h _ {t - 1} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {3, t} \right\} + \frac {1}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} h _ {t - 1} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {3, t} \right\} - \frac {8}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} h _ {t} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {3, t} \right\} \\ = \frac {8}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \left(\left(h _ {t - 1} - h _ {t - 1} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {1, t} \right\} - h _ {t - 1} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {2, t} \right\}\right) - \left(h _ {t} - h _ {t} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {1, t} \right\} - h _ {t} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {2, t} \right\}\right)\right) \\ = \frac {8}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \left(h _ {t} - h _ {t - 1}\right) \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {1, t} \cup \mathcal {J} _ {2, t} \right\} + \frac {8}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \left(h _ {t - 1} - h _ {t}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Here, the second line holds true since, for all  $t$ , one has (i)  $\mathcal{I}_{1,t} \cup \mathcal{I}_{2,t} \cup \mathcal{I}_{3,t} = \mathbb{R}^d$ , and (ii)  $\mathcal{I}_{1,t}$ ,  $\mathcal{I}_{2,t}$  and  $\mathcal{I}_{3,t}$  are disjoint. Substituting this into (151), we arrive at

$$
\begin{array}{l} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {3, t} \right\} \right] \leq 2 \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t - 1} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {3, t} \right\} \right] \\ \leq \frac {8}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \left(\underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {1, t} \cup \mathcal {J} _ {2, t} \right\} \right] - \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t - 1} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {1, t} \cup \mathcal {J} _ {2, t} \right\} \right]\right) \\ + \frac {8}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \left(\underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t - 1} \right] - \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t} \right]\right). \tag {152} \\ \end{array}
$$

In order to further bound (152), we make note of a few basic facts. Firstly, the identity below holds:

$$
\underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t} \right] = \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} \left(Y _ {t}\right)}{p _ {t} \left(Y _ {t}\right)} \right] = \underset {Y _ {t} \sim p _ {t}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} \left(Y _ {t}\right)}{p _ {t} \left(Y _ {t}\right)} \right] = 1, \quad 2 \leq t \leq T.
$$

Secondly, by defining the set

$$
\mathcal {E} _ {t} := \left\{y: q _ {t} (y) > \exp (- c _ {6} d \log T) \right\}, \quad 2 \leq t \leq T, \tag {153}
$$

we can show that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {t} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {t}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {1, t} \right\} \right] \leq \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {t} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {t} \right\} \right] = \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {t} \sim p _ {t}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {t} (Y _ {t})}{p _ {t} (Y _ {t})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {t} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {t} \right\} \right] \\ = \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \mathbb {P} _ {Y _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left\{Y _ {t} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {t} \right\} = \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \mathbb {P} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left\{X _ {t} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {t} \right\} \\ \leq \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \mathbb {P} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left\{X _ {t} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {t} \text {a n d} \| X _ {t} \| _ {2} \leq T ^ {2 c _ {R} + 2} \right\} + \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \mathbb {P} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left\{\| X _ {t} \| _ {2} > T ^ {2 c _ {R} + 2} \right\} \\ \leq \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \int_ {x _ {t}: q _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \leq \exp \left(- c _ {6} d \log T\right), \left\| x _ {t} \right\| _ {2} \leq T ^ {2 c _ {R} + 2}} q _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t} + T \exp \left(- c _ {6} d \log T\right) \\ \leq T \left(2 T ^ {2 c _ {R} + 2}\right) ^ {d} \exp \left(- c _ {6} d \log T\right) + T \exp \left(- c _ {6} d \log T\right) \leq \exp \left(- \frac {c _ {6}}{2} d \log T\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the penultimate line comes from (62), and the last inequality holds true as long as  $c_6$  is large enough. Plugging the preceding two results into (152), we reach

$$
\begin{array}{l} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {4} \right\} \right] \leq \frac {8}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \left(h _ {t} - h _ {t - 1}\right) \mathbb {1} \left\{y _ {t} \in \mathcal {E} _ {t}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {1, t} \right\} \right] \\ + \frac {8}{7} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {2, t} \right\} \right] + \exp \left(- \frac {c _ {6}}{2} d \log T\right). \tag {154} \\ \end{array}
$$

As it turns out, the sum w.r.t. the set  $\mathcal{I}_{1,t}$  and the sum w.r.t. the set  $\mathcal{I}_{2,t}$  in (154) can be controlled respectively using the same arguments as for  $\mathcal{I}_1$  and  $\mathcal{I}_3$  to derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \left(h _ {t} - h _ {t - 1}\right) \mathbb {1} \left\{y _ {t} \in \mathcal {E} _ {t}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {1, t} \right\} \right] \lesssim \frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T} + \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {2}} + \sqrt {d \log^ {3} T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}} + (d \log T) \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}}, \\ \sum_ {t = 2} ^ {T} \underset {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ h _ {t} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {J} _ {2, t} \right\} \right] \lesssim \sqrt {d \log^ {3} T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}} + (d \log T) \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}}; \\ \end{array}
$$

we omit the arguments here for the sake of brevity. Therefore, we have proven that

$$
\mathbb {E} _ {Y _ {T} \sim p _ {T}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} \left(Y _ {1}\right)}{p _ {1} \left(Y _ {1}\right)} \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E}, Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {I} _ {4} \right\} \right] \lesssim \frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T} + \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {2}} + \sqrt {d \log^ {3} T} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}} + (d \log T) \varepsilon_ {\text {J a c o b i}}. \tag {155}
$$

Putting all this together. Taking (147), (149) and (155) together, we establish the advertised result.

# C Proofs of auxiliary lemmas for the DDPM-type sampler

# C.1 Proof of Lemma 8

For notational simplicity, we find it helpful to define, for any constant  $\gamma \in [0,1]$

$$
x _ {t} (\gamma) := \gamma x _ {t - 1} + (1 - \gamma) \widehat {x} _ {t} \quad \text {a n d} \quad \widehat {x} _ {t} := \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} x _ {t}. \tag {156}
$$

Step 1: decomposing the target distribution  $p_{X_{t-1} \mid X_t}(x_{t-1} \mid x_t)$ . With this piece of notation in mind, we can recall the forward process (4) and calculate: for any  $x_{t-1}, x_t \in \mathbb{R}^d$ ,

$$
p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t})
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {1}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t})} p _ {X _ {t - 1}, X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1}, x _ {t}) = \frac {1}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t})} \exp \left(\log p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t - 1}) + \log p _ {X _ {t} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t} \mid x _ {t - 1})\right) \\ = \frac {1}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t})} \exp \left(\log p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (\widehat {x} _ {t}) + \int_ {0} ^ {1} \left[ \nabla \log p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t} (\gamma)) \right] ^ {\top} (x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}) d \gamma + \log p _ {X _ {t} | X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t} | x _ {t - 1})\right) \\ = \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (\widehat {x} _ {t})}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t})} \exp \left(\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \mathrm {d} \gamma \int_ {x _ {0}} \frac {\nabla p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) \mid x _ {0}\right) p _ {X _ {0}} (x _ {0})}{p _ {X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma)\right)} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} + \log p _ {X _ {t} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t} \mid x _ {t - 1}\right)\right), \tag {157} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the penultimate line comes from the fundamental theorem of calculus. In particular, the exponent in (157) consists of a term that satisfies

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \mathrm {d} \gamma \int_ {x _ {0}} \frac {\nabla p _ {X _ {t - 1}} | _ {X _ {0}} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) \mid x _ {0}\right) p _ {X _ {0}} \left(x _ {0}\right)}{p _ {X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma)\right)} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ = \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \mathrm {d} \gamma \int_ {x _ {0}} \frac {\nabla p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) \mid x _ {0}\right)}{p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) \mid x _ {0}\right)} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ = - \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \mathrm {d} \gamma \int_ {x _ {0}} \frac {x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ =: - \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \int_ {0} ^ {1} g _ {t - 1} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) \mathrm {d} \gamma , \tag {158} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second line holds since  $p_{X_0 \mid X_{t-1}}(x_0 \mid x_t(\gamma)) p_{X_{t-1}}(x_t(\gamma)) = p_{X_{t-1} \mid X_0}(x_t(\gamma) \mid x_0) p_{X_0}(x_0)$ , and we remind the reader of the definition of  $g_t(\cdot)$  in (36).

To continue, it is then seen from the fundamental theorem of calculus that

$$
g _ {t - 1} \big (x _ {t} (\gamma) \big) = g _ {t - 1} \big (\widehat {x} _ {t} \big) + \int_ {0} ^ {1} J _ {t - 1} \Big ((1 - \tau) \widehat {x} _ {t} + \tau x _ {t} (\gamma) \Big) \big (x _ {t} (\gamma) - \widehat {x} _ {t} \big) \mathrm {d} \tau ,
$$

where  $J_{t - 1}(x)\coloneqq \frac{\partial g_{t - 1}(x)}{\partial x}\in \mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$  is the associated Jacobian matrix. As a consequence, we can show that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \mathrm {d} \gamma \int_ {x _ {0}} \frac {\nabla p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) \mid x _ {0}\right) p _ {X _ {0}} \left(x _ {0}\right)}{p _ {X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma)\right)} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ = - \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}} \left\{\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) + \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \int_ {0} ^ {1} J _ {t - 1} \left((1 - \tau) \widehat {x} _ {t} + \tau x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \mathrm {d} \tau \mathrm {d} \gamma \right\} \\ = - \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}} \left\{\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) + \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {1} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \gamma J _ {t - 1} \left((1 - \tau) \widehat {x} _ {t} + \tau x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) \mathrm {d} \tau \mathrm {d} \gamma \right] \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \right\}. \tag {159} \\ \end{array}
$$

Combining (157) and (159) allows us to rewrite the target quantity  $p_{X_{t-1} \mid X_t}(x_{t-1} \mid x_t)$  as:

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) \\ = \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (\widehat {x} _ {t})}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t})} \exp \left((x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}) ^ {\top} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \mathrm {d} \gamma \int_ {x _ {0}} \frac {\nabla p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (\widetilde {x} _ {t} \mid x _ {0}) p _ {X _ {0}} (x _ {0})}{p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (\widetilde {x} _ {t})} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} + \log p _ {X _ {t} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t} \mid x _ {t - 1})\right) \\ = \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (\widehat {x} _ {t})}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t})} \exp \left(- \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t - 1} (\widehat {x} _ {t}) + \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {1} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \gamma J _ {t - 1} ((1 - \tau) \widehat {x} _ {t} + \tau x _ {t} (\gamma)) \mathrm {d} \tau \mathrm {d} \gamma \right] (x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) \\ \left. - \frac {\alpha_ {t} \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} - \frac {d}{2} \log (2 \pi (1 - \alpha))\right), \tag {160} \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have also used the fact that conditional on  $X_{t} \mid X_{t - 1} = x_{t - 1} \sim \mathcal{N}\big(\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}} x_{t - 1}, (1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t})I_{d}\big)$ . Note that the pre-factor  $\frac{px_{t - 1}(\widehat{x}_t)}{pX_t(x_t)}$  in the above display is independent from the specific value of  $x_{t - 1}$ .

Step 2: controlling the exponent in (160). Consider now any  $(x_{t}, x_{t-1}) \in \mathcal{E}$  (cf. (80)). In order to further simplify the exponent in the display (160), we make the following claims:

(a) for any  $x$  that can be written as  $x = w x_{t-1} + (1 - w) x_t / \sqrt{\alpha_t}$  for some  $w \in [0,1]$ , the Jacobian matrix  $J_{t-1}(x) = \frac{\partial g_{t-1}(x)}{\partial x}$  obeys

$$
\left\| J _ {t - 1} (x) - I \right\| \lesssim d \log T; \tag {161a}
$$

(b) in addition, one has

$$
\left. \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \left\| \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) g _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) - \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) g _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \lesssim \frac {d \log^ {2} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}. \right. \tag {161b}
$$

$$
\left\| \frac {g _ {t - 1} (\widehat {x} _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {g _ {t} (x _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right\| _ {2} \lesssim (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \left(\frac {d \log T}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3 / 2}, \tag {161c}
$$

Assuming the validity of these claims (which will be established in Appendix C.1.1) and recalling the definition of  $\mu_t(\cdot)$  in (74b), we can use (160) together with a little algebra to obtain

$$
p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) = f _ {0} \left(x _ {t}\right) \exp \left(- \frac {\alpha_ {t} \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} + \zeta_ {t} \left(x _ {t - 1}, x _ {t}\right)\right) \tag {162}
$$

for some function  $f_{0}(\cdot)$  and some residual term  $\zeta_t(x_{t-1}, x_t)$  obeying

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \zeta_ {t} \left(x _ {t - 1}, x _ {t}\right) \right| \lesssim \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \sup  _ {x: \exists w \in [ 0, 1 ] \text {s . t .} x = (1 - w) \widehat {x} _ {t} + w x _ {t - 1}} \left\| J _ {t - 1} (x) \right\| \\ + \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} \left\| \frac {\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}) (\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}) (x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right\| _ {2} \\ \lesssim \frac {\left(d \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \log T\right) d \log T}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}} + \sqrt {d \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \log T} \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \left(\frac {d \log T}{\alpha_ {t} - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \\ \lesssim \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) d ^ {2} \log^ {2} T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} + d ^ {2} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \log^ {2} T \asymp \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) d ^ {2} \log^ {2} T}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the penultimate line makes use of the assumption  $(x_{t},x_{t - 1})\in \mathcal{E}$  (cf. (80)), and the last inequality holds since  $\alpha_{t}\geq 1 / 2$  (cf. (22)).

Step 3: approximating the function  $f_{0}(x_{t})$ . To finish up, it remains to quantify the function  $f_{0}(\cdot)$  in (162). Note that for any  $x_{t}$  obeying  $p_{X_t}(x_t) \geq \exp \left(-\frac{1}{2} c_6 d \log T\right)$ , it is easily seen that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {x _ {t - 1}: \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \notin \mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} = \frac {\int_ {x _ {t - 1} : \left(x _ {t} , x _ {t - 1}\right) \notin \mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {t}} \mid X _ {t - 1} \left(x _ {t} \mid x _ {t - 1}\right) p _ {X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t - 1}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1}}{p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right)} \\ \leq \frac {\frac {1}{(2 \pi (1 - \alpha_ {t})) ^ {d / 2}} \int_ {x _ {t - 1} : (x _ {t} , x _ {t - 1}) \notin \mathcal {E}} \exp \left(- \frac {\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {t - 1} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1}}{\exp \left(- \frac {1}{2} c _ {6} d \log T\right)} \\ \leq \frac {\exp (- c _ {3} d \log T)}{\exp (- \frac {1}{2} c _ {6} d \log T)} \leq \exp \left(- \frac {1}{4} c _ {6} d \log T\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

provided that  $c_{3} \geq 3c_{6} / 4$ . This means that

$$
1 \geq \int_ {x _ {t - 1}: (x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}) \in \mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \geq 1 - \exp \left(- \frac {1}{4} c _ {6} d \log T\right). \tag {163}
$$

Moreover, for any  $(x_{t},x_{t - 1})\in \mathcal{E}$  , one has

$$
\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} \big \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \big \| _ {2} = \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} \bigg \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \mathbb {E} \left[ x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \bigg \| _ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \geq \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right)} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \\ \geq c _ {3} \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} \cdot \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T} - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} 6 \bar {c} _ {5} \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) \log T} \\ \geq \frac {1}{2} c _ {3} \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first identity comes from the definition (74b) of  $\mu_t^\star(x_t)$ , the penultimate line makes use of the result (43a) in Lemma 1, and the last inequality is valid as long as  $c_3$  is sufficiently large. This in turn allows one to derive

$$
\frac {1}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {d / 2}} \int_ {x _ {t - 1}: \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \in \mathcal {E}} \exp \left(- \frac {\alpha_ {t} \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)}\right) d x _ {t - 1} \geq 1 - \exp \left(- c _ {3} d \log T\right). \tag {164}
$$

In addition, by virtue of (162), the integral in (163) can be respectively bounded from above and from below as follows:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {x _ {t - 1}: \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \in \mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \\ = \frac {f _ {0} \left(x _ {t}\right) \int_ {x _ {t - 1} : \left(x _ {t} , x _ {t - 1}\right) \in \mathcal {E}} \exp \left(- \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \zeta_ {t} \left(x _ {t - 1} , x _ {t}\right)\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1}}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {- d / 2} \int_ {x _ {t - 1}} \exp \left(- \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1}} \\ \leq \frac {f _ {0} \left(x _ {t}\right) \int_ {x _ {t - 1} : \left(x _ {t} , x _ {t - 1}\right) \in \mathcal {E}} \exp \left(- \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \zeta_ {t} \left(x _ {t - 1} , x _ {t}\right)\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1}}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {- d / 2} \int_ {x _ {t - 1} : \left(x _ {t} , x _ {t - 1}\right) \in \mathcal {E}} \exp \left( \right.- \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left\| \right. x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \| _ {2} ^ {2}\left. \right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1}} \\ \lesssim \frac {f _ {0} (x _ {t})}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {- d / 2}} \exp \left\{O \left(d ^ {2} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) \log^ {2} T\right) \right\} \\ \end{array}
$$

and

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {x _ {t - 1}: \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \in \mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \\ \geq \left(1 - \exp \left(- c _ {3} d \log T\right)\right) \frac {f _ {0} \left(x _ {t}\right) \int_ {x _ {t - 1} : \left(x _ {t} , x _ {t - 1}\right) \in \mathcal {E}} \exp \left(- \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \zeta_ {t} \left(x _ {t - 1} , x _ {t}\right)\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1}}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {- d / 2} \int_ {x _ {t - 1} : \left(x _ {t} , x _ {t - 1}\right) \in \mathcal {E}} \exp \left( \right.- \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left\| \right. x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \| _ {2} ^ {2}\left. \right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1}} \\ \geq \left(1 - \exp \left(- c _ {3} d \log T\right)\right) \frac {f _ {0} \left(x _ {t}\right)}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {- d / 2}} \exp \left\{- O \left(d ^ {2} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) \log^ {2} T\right) \right\}. \\ \end{array}
$$

These taken collectively with (163) allow one to demonstrate that

$$
\max \left\{\frac {f _ {0} (x _ {t})}{(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}) ^ {- d / 2}}, \frac {(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}) ^ {- d / 2}}{f _ {0} (x _ {t})} \right\} = \exp \left\{O \left(d ^ {2} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) \log^ {2} T\right) \right\} = 1 + O \left(d ^ {2} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) \log^ {2} T\right),
$$

with the proviso that  $d^{2}\bigl (\frac{1 - \alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t} - \overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigr)\log^{2}T\lesssim 1.$

Combining this with (162) concludes the proof of Lemma 8, as long as the two claims in (161) are valid (to be justified in Appendix C.1.1).

# C.1.1 Proof of auxiliary claims (161) in Lemma 8

Proof of relation (161a). Recall from (38) that

$$
J _ {t - 1} (x) - I = \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \mathbb {E} \left[ x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1} = x \right] \left(\mathbb {E} \left[ x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1} = x \right]\right) ^ {\top}
$$

$$
- \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} X _ {0}\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} X _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mid X _ {t - 1} = x \right].
$$

Recognizing that

$$
\left|\left| \mathbb {E} \big [ Z Z ^ {\top} \big ] - \mathbb {E} [ Z ] \mathbb {E} [ Z ] ^ {\top} \right|\right| = \left| \right.\left| \right. \mathbb {E} \Big [ \big (Z - \mathbb {E} [ Z ] \big) \big (Z - \mathbb {E} [ Z ] \big) ^ {\top} \left. \right]\left. \right|\left. \right| \leq \left|\left| \mathbb {E} \big [ Z Z ^ {\top} \big ] \right|\right| \leq \mathbb {E} \big [ \left|\left| Z Z ^ {\top} \right|\right| \big ] = \mathbb {E} \big [ \| Z \| _ {2} ^ {2} \big ]
$$

for any random vector  $Z$ , we can readily obtain

$$
\left\| J _ {t - 1} (x) - I \right\| \leq \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} X _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \mid X _ {t - 1} = x \right]. \tag {165}
$$

When  $(x_{t},x_{t - 1})\in \mathcal{E}$ , it follows from Lemma 2 that

$$
- \log p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (x) \leq c _ {6} d \log T \tag {166}
$$

for any  $x$  lying in the line segment connecting  $x_{t-1}$  and  $\widehat{x}_t$ . With this result in place, taking (165) together with the bound (43b) in Lemma 1 immediately leads to

$$
\left\| J _ {t - 1} (x) - I \right\| \lesssim \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \cdot \left\{d \big (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1} \big) \log T \right\} \asymp d \log T
$$

for any  $x$  lying within the line segment between  $x_{t - 1}$  and  $x_{t} / \sqrt{\alpha_{t}}$ , as claimed.

Proof of relation (161b). To prove this result, we observe that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| (1 - \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}) \frac {(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}) ^ {\top} g _ {t} (x _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right| \leq \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 + \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} \frac {\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} \mathbb {E} [ \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} X _ {0} \| _ {2} | X _ {t} = x _ {t} ]}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \\ \lesssim \frac {\log T}{T} \cdot \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T} \cdot \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) \log T} \lesssim \frac {d \log^ {2} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last line comes from Lemma 1 as well as the basic property (39a) about the learning rates.

Proof of relation (161c). To begin with, the triangle inequality together with the fact  $\overline{\alpha}_t = \prod_{k=1}^t \alpha_k$  gives

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}) (\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}) (x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right\| _ {2} \\ \leq \left\| \frac {\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}) (x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}) (x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}}{\sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})}} \right\| _ {2} \\ + \left\| \left(\frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1})} - \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2}. \tag {167} \\ \end{array}
$$

Let us first consider the last term in (167). According to Lemma 1, given that  $-\log p_{X_t}(x_t) \leq \frac{1}{2} c_6 d \log T$  for some constant  $c_6 > 0$ , one has

$$
\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \lesssim \sqrt {d (1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) \log T}. \tag {168}
$$

This in turn reveals that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \left(\frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})} - \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}) (x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \\ \lesssim \left| \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})} - \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right| \cdot \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) \log T} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \succ \frac {(1 - \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}) (1 + \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1} \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}})}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) \log T} \\ \succ \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{(\alpha_ {t} - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) ^ {3 / 2}} \sqrt {d \log T}, \tag {169} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality makes use of the properties (39).

Next, we turn attention to the first term in (167), which relies on the following claim.

Claim 2. Consider any point  $x_{t}$  obeying  $-\log p_{X_t}(x_t) \leq c_6 d \log T$  for some large constant  $c_6 > 0$ . One has

$$
p _ {X _ {t - 1}} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) = \left(1 + O \left(\frac {d \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right)\right) p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right), \tag {170a}
$$

In addition, by defining the following set

$$
\mathcal {E} _ {1} := \left\{x: \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x \| _ {2} \leq c _ {4} \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) \log T} \right\}
$$

for some large enough constant  $c_4 > 0$ , we have

$$
\frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (\widehat {x} _ {t} \mid x _ {0})}{p _ {X _ {t} \mid X _ {0}} (x _ {t} \mid x _ {0})} = 1 + O \left(\frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right), \quad \text {i f} x _ {0} \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}, \tag {170b}
$$

$$
\frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (\widehat {x} _ {t} \mid x _ {0})}{p _ {X _ {t} \mid X _ {0}} (x _ {t} \mid x _ {0})} \leq \exp \left(\frac {1 6 c _ {1} \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \log T}{(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) T}\right), \quad \text {i f} x _ {0} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}. \tag {170c}
$$

As an immediate consequence of this claim, one has

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac{p_{X_{0}\mid X_{t - 1}}(x_{0}\mid\widehat{x}_{t})}{p_{X_{0}\mid X_{t}}(x_{0}\mid x_{t})} = \frac{p_{X_{t - 1}\mid X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\mid x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\mid X_{0}}(x_{t}\mid x_{0})}\cdot \frac{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}{p_{X_{t - 1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})} = \left(1 + O\bigg(\frac{d(1 - \alpha_{t})\log T}{1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t - 1}}\bigg)\right)\frac{p_{X_{t - 1}\mid X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\mid x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\mid X_{0}}(x_{t}\mid x_{0})} \\ \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} = 1 + O \left(\frac {d \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right), & \text {i f} x _ {0} \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}, \\ \leq \exp \left(\frac {1 6 c _ {1} \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \log T}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) T}\right), & \text {i f} x _ {0} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}. \end{array} \right. \tag {171} \\ \end{array}
$$

In turn, this allows one to deduce that

$$
\left\| \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \leq \int_ {x _ {0} \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}} \left| p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) - p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \right| \cdot \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ + \int_ {x _ {0} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} \left| p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) - p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \right| \cdot \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \leq \int_ {x _ {0}} O \left(\frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ + \int_ {x _ {0} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} \exp \left(\frac {1 6 c _ {1} \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \log T}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) T}\right) p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}) \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \leq O \left(\frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {0} \right] \\ + \int_ {x _ {0} \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} \exp \left(\frac {2 0 c _ {1} \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \log T}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) T}\right) p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Invoking (43a) in Lemma 1 as well as similar arguments for (118) (assuming that  $c_4$  is sufficiently large), we arrive at

$$
\frac {1}{\sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}\right)} \left\| \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3 / 2} (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \sqrt {1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \log^ {3 / 2} T}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})} \cdot \frac {1}{T ^ {c _ {0}}} \\ \asymp \frac {d ^ {3 / 2} (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log^ {3 / 2} T}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) ^ {3 / 2}}, \tag {172} \\ \end{array}
$$

where  $c_{0}$  is some large enough constant, and we have also made use of the properties in (39).

Substituting (169) and (172) into (167) readily concludes the proof.

Proof of Claim 2. Let us make the following observations: for any  $x_0 \in \mathcal{E}_1$ , one has

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (\widehat {x} _ {t} \mid x _ {0})}{p _ {X _ {t} \mid X _ {0}} (x _ {t} \mid x _ {0})} = \left(\frac {1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) ^ {d / 2} \exp \left(\frac {\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} - \frac {\| \widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})}\right) \\ = \exp \left\{\left(1 + o (1)\right) \frac {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1} (1 - \alpha_ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \cdot \frac {d}{2} \right\} \exp \left(- \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})}\right) \\ = \exp \left\{O \left(\frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})}\right) \right\} \\ = \exp \left\{O \left(\frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} + \frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) \right\} \\ = 1 + O \left(\frac {d \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right), \tag {173} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second line holds since

$$
\log \frac {1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} = \log \left(1 + \frac {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1} (1 - \alpha_ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) = \big (1 + o (1) \big) \frac {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1} (1 - \alpha_ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}},
$$

and we have also made use of (39) (so that  $\frac{d(1 - \alpha_t)\log T}{1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t - 1}} = o(1)$  under our assumption on  $T$ ). Additionally, for any  $x_0\notin \mathcal{E}_1$ , it follows from the argument for (110) that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (\widehat {x} _ {t} \mid x _ {0})}{p _ {X _ {t} \mid X _ {0}} (x _ {t} \mid x _ {0})} \leq \exp \left(\frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t})}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})} + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) ^ {2}}\right) \\ \leq \exp \left(\frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \big \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \big \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 c _ {4} ^ {2} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) ^ {2} \log T} + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \big \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \big \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) ^ {2}}\right) \\ \leq \exp \left(\frac {8 c _ {1} \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \log T}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) T}\right) \leq \exp \left(\frac {1 6 c _ {1} \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \log T}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) T}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last line comes from (39b) and (39c). Taking the above bounds together and invoking the same calculation as in (116), (118) and (121), we reach

$$
p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (\widehat {x} _ {t}) = \left(1 + O \Big (\frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \Big)\right) p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}),
$$

thus concluding the proof of Claim 2.

# C.2 Proof of Lemma 9

First, recognizing that  $X_{t}$  follows a Gaussian distribution when conditioned on  $X_{t - 1} = x_{t - 1}$ , we can derive

$$
\log p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) = \log \frac {p _ {X _ {t} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t} \mid x _ {t - 1}) p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t - 1})}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t})}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \log \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t - 1})}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t})} + \frac {\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} x _ {t - 1} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} - \frac {d}{2} \log \left(2 \pi (1 - \alpha_ {t})\right) \\ \leq \log \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t - 1}\right)}{p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right)} + T \left(\left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + 1\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality makes use of the properties (39) about  $\alpha_{t}$  (recall that  $\widehat{x}_t$  has been defined in (77)). Some direct calculations then yield

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t - 1}\right)}{p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right)} = \log \frac {\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0}} \left(x _ {0}\right) \exp \left(- \frac {\left\| x _ {t - 1} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}\right)}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}}{\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0}} \left(x _ {0}\right) \exp \left( \right.- \frac {\left\| \right. x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}}2 \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right)\left. \right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}} - \frac {d}{2} \log \left(\frac {1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) \\ \leq \sup  _ {x _ {0}: \| x _ {0} \| _ {2} \leq T ^ {c} R} \left\{\frac {\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} - \frac {\| x _ {t - 1} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})} \right\} + \frac {d}{2} \log 2 \\ \leq \sup  _ {x _ {0}: \| x _ {0} \| _ {2} \leq T ^ {c} R} \frac {\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} + \frac {d}{2} \log 2 \\ \leq \sup  _ {x _ {0}: \| x _ {0} \| _ {2} \leq T ^ {c _ {R}}} \frac {\| x _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \| x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} + \frac {d}{2} \log 2 \\ \leq 2 T \left(\| x _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second line makes use of the properties in (39). Combining the above two relations, we arrive at

$$
\log p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \leq 2 T \left(\left\| x _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}}\right). \tag {174}
$$

We then turn attention to the conditional distribution  $p_{Y_{t-1}^*} \mid Y_t$ . First, it follows from (76b) that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log \frac {1}{p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} \mid Y _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right)} = \frac {\alpha_ {t} \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} + \frac {d}{2} \log \left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) (175) \\ \leq T \left(\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \| x _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}}\right) + d \log T, (176) \\ \end{array}
$$

with  $\mu_t(x_t)$  defined in (74b). To see why the last inequality follows, we recall from (74b) that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \leq 2 \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \left\| \widehat {x} _ {t} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \\ = 2 \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})}\right) ^ {2} \left\| \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \\ \leq 2 \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {2 (1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{\alpha_ {t} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) ^ {2}} \sup  _ {x _ {0}: \| x _ {0} \| _ {2} \leq T ^ {c} R} \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \\ \leq 2 \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {6 4 c _ {1} ^ {2} \log^ {2} T}{T ^ {2}} \left(2 \| x _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t} T ^ {2 c _ {R}}\right) \\ \leq 2 \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \| x _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}}, \tag {177} \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have invoked the properties (39).

Putting everything together, we arrive at the advertised crude bound:

$$
\log \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t})}{p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} \mid Y _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t})} \leq 2 T \left(\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \| x _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}}\right).
$$

# C.3 Proof of Lemma 10

To begin with, we make the observation that

$$
\mathbb {E} _ {x _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \mathsf {K L} \left(p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (\cdot \mid x _ {t}) \| p _ {Y _ {t - 1} \mid Y _ {t}} (\cdot \mid x _ {t})\right) \right] - \mathbb {E} _ {x _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \mathsf {K L} \left(p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (\cdot \mid x _ {t}) \| p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {*} \mid Y _ {t}} (\cdot \mid x _ {t})\right) \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \int p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) \log \frac {p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star}} \mid Y _ {t} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t})}{p _ {Y _ {t - 1} \mid Y _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t})} d x _ {t - 1} d x _ {t} \\ = \int p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left(\left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} - \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}\right) d x _ {t - 1} d x _ {t} \\ = \underbrace {\int p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e} , t} \left(x _ {t}\right) ^ {2} - \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {\top} \left(s _ {t} (x) - s _ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right)\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t}} _ {=: \mathcal {H} _ {t}}, \tag {178} \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have used (76a) and (76b) as well as the definition (48) of  $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{score},t}(x)$ . Everything comes down to proving that

$$
\mathcal {H} _ {t} \lesssim \exp \left(- \frac {\min  \left\{c _ {3} , c _ {6} \right\}}{8} d \log T\right) + \frac {d \log^ {3} T}{T} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} \left(X _ {t}\right) ^ {2} \right]. \tag {179}
$$

Towards this end, one first notes that the first component of  $\mathcal{H}_t$  obeys

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (x _ {t}) ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} = \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (X _ {t}) ^ {2} \right] \\ \lesssim \frac {\log T}{T} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (X _ {t}) ^ {2} \right], \tag {180} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality comes from (39). We then switch attention to the second component of  $\mathcal{H}_t$ . In view of the distribution of  $Y_{t - 1}^{\star}\mid Y_{t}$  in (76b), we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {\top} \left(s _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) - s _ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ = \int p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \left(p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) - p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} \mid Y _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t})\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t})\right) ^ {\top} \left(s _ {t} (x _ {t}) - s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t})\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ + \int_ {x _ {t}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \left\{\int_ {x _ {t - 1}} p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} \mid Y _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t})\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \right\} \left(s _ {t} (x _ {t}) - s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t})\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \stackrel {\text {(i)}}{=}\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t - 1}\mid X_{t}}(x_{t - 1}\mid x_{t})\biggl(1 - \frac{p_{Y_{t - 1}^{\star}\mid Y_{t}}(x_{t - 1}\mid x_{t})}{p_{X_{t - 1}\mid X_{t}}(x_{t - 1}\mid x_{t})}\biggr)\bigl(x_{t - 1} - \mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\bigr)^{\top}\bigl(s_{t}(x_{t}) - s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\bigr)\mathrm{d}x_{t - 1}\mathrm{d}x_{t} \\ \leq \underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t - 1}\mid X_{t}}(x_{t - 1}\mid x_{t})\Big(1 - \frac{p_{Y_{t - 1}^{\star}\mid Y_{t}}(x_{t - 1}\mid x_{t})}{p_{X_{t - 1}\mid X_{t}}(x_{t - 1}\mid x_{t})}\Big)(x_{t - 1} - \mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t}))^{\top}\big(s_{t}(x_{t}) - s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)\mathrm{d}x_{t - 1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}}_{=: \mathcal{K}_{1}} \\ + \underbrace {\int_ {\mathcal {E} ^ {\mathrm {c}}} p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) \left\{p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) + p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star}} \mid Y _ {t} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \right\} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \left\| s _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) - s _ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t}} _ {=: \mathcal {K} _ {2}}, \tag {181} \\ \end{array}
$$

where (i) holds since  $\mathbb{E}\bigl [Y_{t - 1}^{\star} - \mu_t^\star (Y_t)\mid Y_t\bigr ] = 0$ . This leaves us with two terms to cope with.

- Regarding the term  $\kappa_{1}$ , we can bound

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {K} _ {1} \stackrel {\text {(i)}} {\lesssim} \frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {3} T}{T} \int_ {\mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \stackrel {\mathrm {(i i)}} {\lesssim} \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {3} T}{T} \int_ {\mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) \big \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \big \| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} + \frac {d \log^ {3} T}{T} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \big [ \varepsilon_ {\mathrm {s c o r e}, t} ^ {2} (X _ {t}) \big ] \\ = \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {3} T}{T} \int_ {\mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right)}{p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} \mid Y _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right)} p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} \mid Y _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2} d x _ {t - 1} d x _ {t} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
+ \frac {d \log^ {3} T}{T} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} ^ {2} (X _ {t}) \right]
$$

$$
\stackrel {\mathrm {(i i i)}} {\lesssim} \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {3} T}{T} \Big (1 + O \Big (\frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {3} T}{T} \Big) \Big) \frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t})}{\alpha_ {t}} + \frac {d \log^ {3} T}{T} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \big [ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} ^ {2} (X _ {t}) \big ] + \exp \Big (- \frac {c _ {3}}{4} d \log T \Big)
$$

$$
\stackrel {(\mathrm {i v})} {\lesssim} \frac {d ^ {4} \log^ {4} T}{T ^ {2}} + \frac {d \log^ {3} T}{T} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (X _ {t}) ^ {2} \right]. \tag {182}
$$

Here, (i) comes from (84), (ii) is due to the elementary inequality  $2ab \leq a^2 + b^2$ , (iii) invokes the relation (84) as well as the Gaussian distribution of  $Y_{t-1}^\star \mid Y_t$  in (76b), whereas (iv) applies (39).

- With regards to the term  $\kappa_{2}$ , it follows from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

$$
\mathcal {K} _ {2} \lesssim \sqrt {\mathcal {K} _ {3} \mathcal {K} _ {4}},
$$

where

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {K} _ {3} := \int_ {\mathcal {E} ^ {c}} p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) \left\{p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) + p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {*} \mid Y _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \right\} \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {*} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2} d x _ {t - 1} d x _ {t}; (183a) \\ \mathcal {K} _ {4} := \int_ {\mathcal {E} ^ {\mathrm {c}}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \left\{p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) + p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} \mid Y _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) \right\} \left\| s _ {t} (x _ {t}) - s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \leq 2 \int p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \| s _ {t} (x _ {t}) - s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} = 2 \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (X _ {t}) ^ {2} \right]. (183b) \\ \end{array}
$$

Consequently, it suffices to look at the term  $\kappa_{3}$ . According to (88), we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \lesssim \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} x _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})}\right) ^ {2} \left(\mathbb {E} \Big [ \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \Big ]\right) ^ {2} \\ \lesssim \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} x _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {\log^ {2} T}{T ^ {2}} \left(\| x _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality makes use of (39) and the assumption (21). Combining this with (87) gives

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {\mathcal {E} ^ {c}} p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \lesssim \int_ {\mathcal {E} ^ {\mathrm {c}}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} x _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ + \frac {\log^ {2} T}{T ^ {2}} \int_ {\mathcal {E} ^ {\mathrm {c}}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) \left(\| x _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \lesssim \exp \left(- c _ {3} d \log T\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Additionally, let us decompose

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {\mathcal {E} ^ {\mathrm {c}}} p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {*} \mid Y _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \leq \underbrace{\int_{(x_{t},x_{t - 1}):p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}) <   e^{-0.5c_{6}d\log T}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{Y_{t - 1}^{\star}}|_{Y_{t}}(x_{t - 1}|x_{t})\big||x_{t - 1} - \mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big||_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t - 1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}}_{=: \mathcal{K}_{5}} \\ + \underbrace \int_ {(x _ {t} , x _ {t - 1}) : p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \geq e ^ {- 0 . 5 c _ {6} d \log T} , \| x _ {t} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} > c _ {3} \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}} _ {=: \mathcal {K} _ {6}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) p _ {Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} | Y _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} | x _ {t}) \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {t - 1} \mathrm {d} x _ {t}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Substitution into (183a) yields

$$
\mathcal {K} _ {2} \lesssim \sqrt {\mathcal {K} _ {3} \mathcal {K} _ {4}} \lesssim \sqrt {\left\{\exp \left(- c _ {3} d \log T\right) + \mathcal {K} _ {5} + \mathcal {K} _ {6} \right\} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} \left(X _ {t}\right) ^ {2} \right]}, \tag {184}
$$

thus motivating us to bound  $\kappa_{5}$  and  $\kappa_{6}$  separately.

- Regarding  $\mathcal{K}_6$ , we can use (89) to demonstrate that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {K} _ {6} \leq \int p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (Y _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathbb {1} \left\{\left\| Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (Y _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {c _ {3}}{2} \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T} \right\} \mid Y _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \lesssim \exp \left(- \frac {c _ {3}}{4} d \log T\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

- Turning to  $\kappa_{5}$ , one can invoke (62) to derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {K} _ {5} \leq \int_ {x _ {t}: p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) <   e ^ {- 0. 5 c _ {6} d \log T}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| Y _ {t - 1} ^ {\star} - \mu_ {t} ^ {\star} (Y _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \mid Y _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \leq d \int_ {x _ {t}: p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) <   e ^ {- 0. 5 c _ {6} d \log T}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \mathrm {d} x _ {t} \\ \leq d \int_ {x _ {t}: p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) <   e ^ {- 0. 5 c _ {6} d \log T}, \| x _ {t} \| _ {2} \leq T ^ {c _ {R} + 2}} p _ {X _ {t}} (x _ {t}) \mathrm {d} x _ {t} + \mathbb {P} \left\{\| X _ {t} \| _ {2} \geq T ^ {c _ {R} + 2} \right\} \\ \leq d \exp \left(- \frac {1}{2} c _ {6} d \log T\right) \cdot (2 T ^ {c _ {R} + 2}) ^ {d} + \exp (- c _ {6} d \log T) \\ \lesssim \exp \Big (- \frac {1}{4} c _ {6} d \log T \Big), \\ \end{array}
$$

provided that  $c_{6} > 0$  is large enough.

Combining the above results with (184), we arrive at

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {K} _ {2} \lesssim \sqrt {\left\{\exp \left(- \frac {c _ {3}}{4} d \log T\right) + \exp \left(- \frac {c _ {6}}{4} d \log T\right) \right\} \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e} , t} (X _ {t}) ^ {2} \right]} \\ \lesssim \exp \left(- \frac {\min  \left\{c _ {3} , c _ {6} \right\}}{4} d \log T\right) + \exp \left(- \frac {\min  \left\{c _ {3} , c _ {6} \right\}}{4} d \log T\right) \mathbb {E} _ {X _ {t} \sim q _ {t}} \left[ \varepsilon_ {\text {s c o r e}, t} (X _ {t}) ^ {2} \right]. \tag {185} \\ \end{array}
$$

Taking the above bounds (180), (182) and (185) together with (178) immediately finishes the proof.

# D Analysis for the accelerated deterministic sampler (Theorem 2)

The aim of this section is to establish Theorem 2. The proof follows similar arguments as that of Theorem 1.

# D.1 Proof of Theorem 2

Auxiliary vectors and their properties. Before embarking on the proof, let us introduce several pieces of notation:

$$
\varphi_ {t} (x) := x - u _ {t} (x), \tag {186a}
$$

where

$$
\begin{array}{l} u _ {t} (x) := \left\{\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} - \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {3}} \| g _ {t} (x) \| _ {2} ^ {2} \right\} g _ {t} (x) \\ + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {3}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left(X _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0}\right) \left(X _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} | X _ {t} = x \right] g _ {t} (x) \\ - \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{8 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {3}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| X _ {t} - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \left(X _ {t} - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} X _ {0} - g _ {t} \left(X _ {t}\right)\right) \mid X _ {t} = x \right] (186b) \\ = - \left\{\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2} + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} - \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8} \| s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x) \| _ {2} ^ {2} \right\} s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x) - \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \mathbb {E} \left[ \overline {{W}} _ {t} \overline {{W}} _ {t} ^ {\top} s _ {t} ^ {\star} (X _ {t}) \mid X _ {t} = x \right] \\ - \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{8 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right)} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| \overline {{W}} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \left(\frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}} \bar {W} _ {t} + \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) s _ {t} ^ {\star} \left(X _ {t}\right)\right) \mid X _ {t} = x \right]. (186c) \\ \end{array}
$$

Here, we recall that  $g_{t}(x) = -(1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t})s_{t}^{\star}(x)$  has been defined in (36) and that  $X_{t} = \sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}} X_{0} + \sqrt{1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t}}\overline{W}_{t}$  with  $\overline{W}_t\sim \mathcal{N}(0,I_d)$  (see (6)). In view of Assumption 3 and the fact that the MMSE estimator is the conditional expectation (Hajek, 2015, Section 3.3.1), we have

$$
w _ {t} (x) = \mathbb {E} \left[ \| \overline {{W}} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \left(\frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}} \overline {{W}} _ {t} + (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) s _ {t} ^ {\star} (X _ {t})\right) + \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \overline {{W}} _ {t} \overline {{W}} _ {t} ^ {\top} s _ {t} ^ {\star} (X _ {t}) \mid X _ {t} = x \right],
$$

which taken together with (186) and (27) confirms the following equivalent expression for the sampler (27):

$$
Y _ {t - 1} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} \varphi_ {t} (Y _ {t}) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} Y _ {t} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} u _ {t} (Y _ {t}). \tag {187}
$$

We now single out a useful property about  $u_{t}(\cdot)$ . For any point  $x_{t} \in \mathbb{R}^{d}$  obeying  $-\log p_{X_t}(x_t) \leq c_6 d \log T$  for some large constant  $c_{6} > 0$  (see Lemma 1), one has

$$
\left\| u _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \lesssim (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \left(\frac {d \log T}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \tag {188a}
$$

and one can also write

$$
u _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) = \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right)} g _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) + \xi_ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \tag {188b}
$$

for some residual term  $\xi_t(x_t)$  obeying

$$
\left\| \xi_ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \lesssim (1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2} \left(\frac {d \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3 / 2}. \tag {188c}
$$

To streamline presentation, we leave the proof of (188) to the end of this subsection.

Main steps of the proof. Akin to the proof of Theorem 1, the key idea lies in understanding the transformation  $\Phi_t$  (cf. (27)), or equivalently,  $\varphi_t$  (cf. (186)). There are several objects that play an important role in the analysis, which we single out as follows:

$$
A _ {t} := \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0}; \tag {189a}
$$

$$
B _ {t} := \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}; \tag {189b}
$$

$$
C _ {t} := \frac {1}{(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {4} \mathrm {d} x _ {0}; \tag {189c}
$$

$$
D _ {t} := \frac {1}{(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \left(\left\langle g _ {t} (x), x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\rangle\right) ^ {2} d x _ {0}; \tag {189d}
$$

$$
E _ {t} := \frac {1}{(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \| x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \left\langle g _ {t} (x), x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\rangle \mathrm {d} x _ {0}. \tag {189e}
$$

Here, we suppress the dependency on  $x$  in the above five objects to simplify notation whenever it is clear from the context. In view of Lemma 1 and the properties (39), we have the following bounds:

$$
\left| B _ {t} \right| \leq \left| A _ {t} \right| \lesssim \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \cdot d \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) \log T \asymp d \log T; \tag {190a}
$$

$$
\left| C _ {t} \right| \lesssim \frac {1}{(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) ^ {2}} d ^ {2} \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {2} \log^ {2} T \asymp d ^ {2} \log^ {2} T; \tag {190b}
$$

$$
\left| D _ {t} \right| \leq \frac {\left\| g _ {t} (x) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{\left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \left\| x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} d x _ {0} \lesssim d ^ {2} \log^ {2} T; \tag {190c}
$$

$$
\left| E _ {t} \right| \leq \frac {\left\| g _ {t} (x) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{\left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \left\| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {3} d x _ {0} \lesssim d ^ {2} \log^ {2} T. \tag {190d}
$$

As it turns out, Theorem 2 can be established in a very similar way as in the proof of Theorem 1. In essence, the only step that needs to be changed is to replace (58) in Lemma 4 with (191) in the lemma below.

Lemma 11. Suppose that  $\frac{d^2(1 - \alpha_t)\log T}{\alpha_t - \overline{\alpha}_t} \lesssim 1$ . For every  $x \in \mathbb{R}$  obeying  $-\log p_{X_t}(x) \leq c_6 d\log T$  for some large enough constant  $c_6 > 0$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {p _ {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} x _ {t - 1} (\varphi_ {t} (x))}{p _ {X _ {t}} (x)} = 1 + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) (d + B _ {t} - A _ {t})}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} + O \Big (d ^ {3} \Big (\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \Big) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T \Big) \\ + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \big [ d (d + 2) + (4 + 2 d) (B _ {t} - A _ {t}) - B _ {t} ^ {2} + C _ {t} + 2 D _ {t} - 3 E _ {t} + A _ {t} B _ {t} \big ]. (1 9 1 \mathrm {a}) \\ \end{array}
$$

Moreover, for any random vector  $Y$ , one has

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {p _ {\varphi_ {t} (Y)} (\varphi_ {t} (x))}{p _ {Y} (x)} = 1 + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) (d + B _ {t} - A _ {t})}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} + O \Big (d ^ {6} \Big (\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \Big) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T \Big) \\ + \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{8 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}} \left[ d (d + 2) + (4 + 2 d) \left(B _ {t} - A _ {t}\right) - B _ {t} ^ {2} + C _ {t} + 2 D _ {t} - 3 E _ {t} + A _ {t} B _ {t} \right]. \tag {191b} \\ \end{array}
$$

Here, the quantities  $A_{t},\ldots ,E_{t}$  are defined in (189).

The proof of this lemma can be found in Appendix D.2. Crucially, the terms in (191a) and those in (191b) coincide (except for the residual terms  $O\big(d^{3}\big(\frac{1 - \alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t} - \overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big)^{3}\log^{3}T\big)$  and  $O\big(d^{6}\big(\frac{1 - \alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t} - \overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big)^{3}\log^{3}T\big)\big)$ , which will cancel each other during the subsequent proof.

With Lemma 11 in place, we claim that for every  $t \geq 1$ ,

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(Y _ {t} \in \mathcal {E} _ {t}\right) \geq 1 - (T - t + 1) \exp \left(- c _ {3} d \log T\right) \tag {192}
$$
