for some constant  $c_{3} > 0$ , where the set  $\mathcal{E}_t$  is defined as

$$
\mathcal {E} _ {t} := \left\{y: \left| \frac {p _ {Y _ {t}} (y)}{p _ {X _ {t}} (y)} - 1 \right| \leq c _ {5} (T - t + 1) \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {3}} \right\}
$$

for some constant  $c_5 > 0$ . We leave its proof to the end of this section.

Suppose for the moment that the above claim (192) is valid. Then taking  $t = 1$  leads to

$$
\mathbb {P} (Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}) = \mathbb {P} \left(\left| \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} - 1 \right| \leq \frac {c _ {5} d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {2}}\right) \geq 1 - T \exp \big (- c _ {3} d \log T \big),
$$

which also reveals that  $\int_{y\notin \mathcal{E}_1}p_1(y)\mathrm{d}y\leq T\exp \big(-c_3d\log T\big)$ . Additionally, we make the observation that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} | p _ {1} (y) - q _ {1} (y) | \mathrm {d} y \leq \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} p _ {1} (y) \mathrm {d} y + \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} q _ {1} (y) \mathrm {d} y = \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} p _ {1} (y) \mathrm {d} y + 1 - \int_ {y \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}} q _ {1} (y) \mathrm {d} y \\ \leq \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} p _ {1} (y) \mathrm {d} y + 1 - \int_ {y \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}} p _ {1} (y) \mathrm {d} y + \int_ {y \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}} \left| p _ {1} (y) - q _ {1} (y) \right| \mathrm {d} y \\ = 2 \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} p _ {1} (y) \mathrm {d} y + \int_ {y \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}} \left| p _ {1} (y) - q _ {1} (y) \right| \mathrm {d} y. \\ \end{array}
$$

The above results together with the definition of the total variation distance gives

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathsf {T V} \left(q _ {1}, p _ {1}\right) = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {y \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}} \left| q _ {1} (y) - p _ {1} (y) \right| \mathrm {d} y + \frac {1}{2} \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} \left| q _ {1} (y) - p _ {1} (y) \right| \mathrm {d} y \\ \leq \int_ {y \in \mathcal {E} _ {1}} | q _ {1} (y) - p _ {1} (y) | \mathrm {d} y + \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} p _ {1} (y) \mathrm {d} y \\ = \mathbb {E} _ {Y _ {1} \sim p _ {1}} \left[ \left| \frac {q _ {1} (Y _ {1})}{p _ {1} (Y _ {1})} - 1 \right| \cdot \mathbb {1} \left\{Y _ {1} \in \mathcal {E} _ {1} \right\} \right] + \int_ {y \notin \mathcal {E} _ {1}} p _ {1} (y) d y \\ \leq \frac {c _ {5} d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {2}} + \exp \left(- c _ {3} d \log T\right) \\ \asymp \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {6} T}{T ^ {2}}. \tag {193} \\ \end{array}
$$

This establishes the advertised result in Theorem 2, provided that Claim (192) can be verified.

Proof of properties (188). To justify the above results (188), note that Lemma 1 implies that

$$
\left\| g _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \leq \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| X _ {t} - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \lesssim \sqrt {d (1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) \log T}, \tag {194a}
$$

$$
\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \left\| g _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \lesssim \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \sqrt {d \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) \log T} \asymp (1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2} \sqrt {\frac {d \log T}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}}, \tag {194b}
$$

$$
\frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{\left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}} \left\| g _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| _ {2} \lesssim \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{\left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}} \sqrt {d \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) \log T} \asymp \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2} \frac {\sqrt {d \log T}}{\left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {3 / 2}}, \tag {194c}
$$

$$
\frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) ^ {3}} \| g _ {t} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {3} \lesssim \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) ^ {3}} \left(d (1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}) \log T\right) ^ {3 / 2} \asymp (1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2} \left(\frac {d \log T}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3 / 2}, \tag {194d}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac{(1 - \alpha_{t})^{2}}{(1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left\| \mathbb{E}\left[ \big(x_{t} - \sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}} X_{0}\big)\big(x_{t} - \sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}} X_{0}\big)^{\top}  \mid   X_{t} = x_{t}\right]g_{t}(x_{t})\right\|_{2}\lesssim \frac{(1 - \alpha_{t})^{2}\left\|g_{t}(x_{t})\right\|_{2}}{(1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[ \left\| x_{t} - \sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}} X_{0}\right\|_{2}^{2}  \mid   X_{t} = x_{t}\right] \\ \lesssim (1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2} \left(\frac {d \log T}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3 / 2}, \tag {194e} \\ \end{array}
$$

and

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{\left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right) ^ {3}} \left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} - g _ {t} (X _ {t})\right) \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \right\| _ {2} \\ \leq \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {3}} \left\{\left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {3} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \right\| _ {2} + \left\| g _ {t} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} \left\| \mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \right\| _ {2} \right\} \\ \lesssim \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {d \log T}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3 / 2}. \tag {194f} \\ \end{array}
$$

Substituting the bounds (194) into (186b) immediately establishes (188).

Proof of the claim (192). We would like to prove this claim by induction, for which we start with the base case with  $t = T$ . Recall that  $X_{T} \stackrel{\mathrm{d}}{=} \sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{T}} X_{0} + \sqrt{1 - \overline{\alpha}_{T}} B$  and  $Y_{T} \stackrel{\mathrm{d}}{=} B$  with  $B \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I_{d})$  independent of  $X_{0}$ , and that  $\|X_{0}\|_{2} \leq R$  with  $R = T^{c_{R}}$  for some constant  $c_{R} > 0$ . For large enough  $T$ , it immediately follows from (39d) that

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(Y _ {T} \in \mathcal {E} _ {t}\right) \geq 1 - \exp \left(- c _ {3} d \log T\right) \tag {195}
$$

for some constant  $c_3 > 0$  large enough.

Suppose now that the claim (192) holds for some  $t \geq 2$ , and we wish to prove the claim for  $t - 1$ . We would first like to claim that with probability at least  $1 - (T - t)\exp (-c_3d\log T)$  for some constant  $c_{3} > 0$ , one has

$$
q _ {t} \left(Y _ {t}\right) \geq \exp (- c _ {6} d \log T). \tag {196}
$$

With (196) in place, one sees that Lemma 11 is applicable to  $x = Y_{t}$  with high probability.

Next, consider any  $y$  obeying  $-\log p_{X_t}(y) \leq c_6 d \log T$ . With these bounds (190) in mind, applying relations (191a) and (191b) in Lemma 11 leads to

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {p _ {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} Y _ {t - 1}} (\phi_ {t} (y))}{p _ {Y _ {t}} (y)} \left(\frac {p _ {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} X _ {t - 1}} (\phi_ {t} (y))}{p _ {X _ {t}} (y)}\right) ^ {- 1} = \frac {p _ {\phi_ {t} (Y _ {t})} (\phi_ {t} (y))}{p _ {Y _ {t}} (y)} \left(\frac {p _ {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} X _ {t - 1}} (\phi_ {t} (y))}{p _ {X _ {t}} (y)}\right) ^ {- 1} \\ = 1 + O \left(d ^ {6} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Replacing  $y$  with  $Y_{t}$  in the above display, using the fact that  $Y_{t - 1} = \frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}}\phi_t(Y_t)$ , and invoking the relation (55), we immediately arrive at

$$
\frac {p _ {t - 1} \left(Y _ {t - 1}\right)}{q _ {t - 1} \left(Y _ {t - 1}\right)} = \left\{1 + O \left(d ^ {6} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T\right) \right\}. \frac {p _ {t} \left(Y _ {t}\right)}{q _ {t} \left(Y _ {t}\right)}
$$

with probability exceeding  $1 - (T - t)\exp (-c_3d\log T)$ . This concludes the proof of Claim (192) via standard induction arguments.

# D.2 Proof of Lemma 11

The proof of Lemma 11 is derived in a very similar way to the proof of Lemma 4, as detailed below. For notational simplicity, we shall abbreviate

$$
u = u _ {t} (x) \quad \text {a n d} \quad z = g _ {t} (x) \tag {197}
$$

whenever it is clear from the context.

# D.2.1 Proof of relation (191a)

Through direct calculations as shown in (127), we can obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} X _ {t - 1}} \left(\varphi_ {t} (x)\right) = p _ {X _ {t}} (x) \left(\frac {1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) ^ {d / 2} \\ \cdot \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \exp \left(- \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} - \frac {\| u \| _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 u ^ {\top} (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0})}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}. \qquad (1 9 8) \\ \end{array}
$$

Moreover, similar to the analysis of Lemma 4, we focus our attention on the set given  $x$ :

$$
\mathcal {E} := \left\{x _ {0}: \left\| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \leq 5 c _ {5} \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) \log T} \right\}, \tag {199}
$$

which allows us to derive, for some numerical constant  $c_{8} > 0$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \exp \Big (- \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} - \frac {\| u \| _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 u ^ {\top} (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0})}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \Big) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ \begin{array}{l} = O \left(\exp (- c _ {8} c _ {5} ^ {2} d \log T)\right) + \int_ {x _ {0} \in \mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \exp \left(- \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} - \frac {\| u \| _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 u ^ {\top} (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0})}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ =: \mathrm {R H S} \end{array} \\ \end{array}
$$

To further control the right-hand side above, recall that the learning rates are selected such that  $\frac{1 - \alpha_t}{1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t-1}} \leq \frac{4c_1\log T}{T}$  for  $1 < t \leq T$  (see (39b)). In view of the Taylor expansion  $e^{-x} = 1 - x + \frac{1}{2}x^2 + O(x^3)$  for  $x \leq 1/2$ , we can derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {R H S} = O \left(\exp \left(- c _ {8} c _ {5} ^ {2} d \log T\right)\right) \\ + \int_ {x _ {0} \in \mathcal {E}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \Bigg \{1 - \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \big \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \big \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} - \frac {\frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{4 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \| z \| _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 u ^ {\top} (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0})}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \\ + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \left(\left\| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} - z ^ {\top} \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right)\right) ^ {2} + O \left(d ^ {3} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T\right) \Bigg \} d x _ {0}. \tag {201} \\ \end{array}
$$

In order to see this, we recall the property of  $u$  (cf. (188)) as

$$
\left\| u - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t})} z \right\| _ {2} \leq O \left((1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2} \left(\frac {d \log T}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3 / 2}\right). \tag {202}
$$

As a consequence, for any  $x_0 \in \mathcal{E}$  we have

$$
\frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} = O \Big (d \Big (\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \Big) \log T \Big)
$$

and

$$
\frac {\| u \| _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 u ^ {\top} \big (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \big)}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} = \frac {\frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{4 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \| z \| _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 u ^ {\top} \big (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \big)}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} + O \left(d ^ {2} \Big (\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \Big) ^ {3} \log^ {2} T\right)
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {z ^ {\top} \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right)}{2 \left(\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right) \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right)} + O \left(d ^ {2} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) ^ {2} \log^ {2} T\right) \\ = O \left(d \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) \log T\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have invoked the properties (39). Taking the above results together and using the following basic properties regarding quantities  $A_{t}, \ldots, E_{t}$  (defined in (189))

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} d x _ {0} = (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) A _ {t}, \\ \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| z \| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} = (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) B _ {t}, \\ \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) u ^ {\top} \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} = \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2} B _ {t} + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \left[ B _ {t} - B _ {t} ^ {2} + D _ {t} - E _ {t} + A _ {t} B _ {t} \right], \\ \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \left(\left\| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} - z ^ {\top} \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right)\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} = (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2} \left[ C _ {t} + D _ {t} - 2 E _ {t} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

we arrive at

$$
(2 0 1) = 1 - \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) (A _ {t} - B _ {t})}{2 (\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \big [ - B _ {t} ^ {2} + C _ {t} + 2 D _ {t} - 3 E _ {t} + A _ {t} B _ {t} \big ] + O \bigg (d ^ {3} \bigg (\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \bigg) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T \bigg).
$$

Once again, we note that integrating over set  $\mathcal{E}$  and over all possible  $x_0$  only incurs a difference at most as large as  $O\big(\exp (-c_8c_5^2 d\log T)\big)$ . Putting all this together establishes the advertised result (191a).

# D.2.2 Proof of relation (191b)

Consider any random vector  $Y$ , and let us invoke again the basic transformation

$$
p _ {\varphi_ {t} (Y)} (\varphi_ {t} (x)) = \det  \left(\frac {\partial \varphi_ {t} (x)}{\partial x}\right) ^ {- 1} p _ {Y} (x),
$$

where  $\frac{\partial\varphi_t(x)}{\partial x}$  denotes the Jacobian matrix. We are then left with controlling the quantity  $\operatorname*{det}\left(\frac{\partial\varphi_t(x)}{\partial x}\right)^{-1}$ . Towards this, let us again recall that the determinant of a matrix satisfies

$$
\det  (I + A) ^ {- 1} = 1 - \operatorname {T r} (A) + \frac {1}{2} \left[ \operatorname {T r} (A) ^ {2} + \| A \| _ {\mathrm {F}} ^ {2} \right] + O \left(d ^ {3} \| A \| ^ {3}\right),
$$

provided that  $d\| A\| \leq c_{20}$  for some small enough constant  $c_{20} > 0$ . This relation leads to

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {\varphi_ {t} (Y)} (\varphi_ {t} (x)) = \det  \left(\frac {\partial \varphi_ {t} (x)}{\partial x}\right) ^ {- 1} p _ {Y} (x) \\ = \left\{1 + \operatorname {T r} \left(\frac {\partial u}{\partial x}\right) + \frac {1}{2} \left[ \operatorname {T r} \left(\frac {\partial u}{\partial x}\right) ^ {2} + \left\| \frac {\partial u}{\partial x} \right\| _ {\mathrm {F}} ^ {2} \right] + O \left(d ^ {3} \left\| \frac {\partial u}{\partial x} \right\|\right) \right\} p _ {Y} (x), \tag {203} \\ \end{array}
$$

where we invoke the definition in (186) that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varphi_ {t} (x) = x - u = x - \left(\frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t})}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} - \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {3}} \| z \| _ {2} ^ {2}\right) z \\ - \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{8 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {3}} \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} z d x _ {0} \\ + \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{8 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {3}} \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \big \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \big \| _ {2} ^ {2} \big (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} - z \big) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

To further control the right-hand side above of the above display, let us first make note of several identities. Proving these identities only requires elementary calculation regarding Gaussian integration and derivatives, which is omitted here for brevity. Specifically, one has

$$
\frac {\partial z}{\partial x} = J _ {t}, \tag {204a}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial x} \| z \| _ {2} ^ {2} z = \| z \| _ {2} ^ {2} J _ {t} + 2 z z ^ {\top} J _ {t}, \tag {204b}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ = \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} d x _ {0} I + 2 \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} d x _ {0} \\ + \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \Big (\Big (\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \big \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \big \| _ {2} ^ {2} \big (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \big) \Big) \Big (\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \big (x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \big) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \Big) ^ {\top} \\ - \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \| x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0}), \tag {204c} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} z = \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} J _ {t} + 2 z z ^ {\top} \\ + \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \left(\left(\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2}\right) z z ^ {\top} - z \left(\int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \| x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} z d x _ {0} = \| z \| _ {2} ^ {2} I + z z ^ {\top} \\ + \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} J _ {t} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \\ + \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x) \left(z ^ {\top} \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right)\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) z ^ {\top} d x _ {0} \\ - \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \left(z ^ {\top} \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right)\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0}. \tag {204e} \\ \end{array}
$$

Equipped with the above relations, we can easily verify that

$$
\left\| \frac {\partial u}{\partial x} \right\| \lesssim \frac {d \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}, \tag {205a}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \operatorname {T r} \left(\frac {\partial u}{\partial x}\right) = \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) (d + B _ {t} - A _ {t})}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \\ + \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{8 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}} \left(d - 2 A _ {t} - A _ {t} ^ {2} + 3 A _ {t} B _ {t} + 2 B _ {t} - 3 B _ {t} ^ {2} + C _ {t} + 4 D _ {t} - 3 E _ {t} - F _ {t}\right), \tag {205b} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial u}{\partial x} \right\| _ {F} ^ {2} = \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2}}{4 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \left\| \frac {\partial z}{\partial x} \right\| _ {F} ^ {2} + O \left(d ^ {5} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T\right) \\ = \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{4 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}} \left(d + 2 \left(B _ {t} - A _ {t}\right) + B _ {t} ^ {2} + F _ {t} - 2 D _ {t}\right) + O \left(d ^ {5} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T\right), \tag {205c} \\ \end{array}
$$

as long as  $d^2\left(\frac{1 - \alpha_t}{\alpha_t - \overline{\alpha}_t}\right)\log T\lesssim 1$  , where we recall the definition of the quantities  $A_{t}$  to  $E_{t}$  in (189), and

$$
F _ {t} (x) := \left\| \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}} \int_ {x _ {0}} p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x - \sqrt {\bar {\alpha} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {\mathrm {F}} ^ {2}. \tag {205d}
$$

Plugging these results into inequality (203) leads to

$$
p _ {\varphi_ {t} (Y)} \left(\varphi_ {t} (x)\right) = p _ {Y} (x) \left\{1 + \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \left(d + B _ {t} - A _ {t}\right)}{2 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right)} + O \left(d ^ {6} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t} - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {3} \log^ {3} T\right) \right.
$$

$$
\left. + \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {2}}{8 \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}} \left[ d (d + 2) + (4 + 2 d) \left(B _ {t} - A _ {t}\right) - B _ {t} ^ {2} + C _ {t} + 2 D _ {t} - 3 E _ {t} + A _ {t} B _ {t} \right] \right\}. \quad (2 0 5 \mathrm {e})
$$

We have thus completed the proof of Lemma 11.

# E Analysis for the accelerated stochastic sampler (Theorems 4)

The proof of Theorem 4 follows similar structure as the proof of Theorem 3. Throughout the proof, we shall employ the notation  $\widehat{x}_t \coloneqq x_t / \sqrt{\alpha_t}$  as before.

# E.1 Proof of Theorem 4

Step 1: expressing the update rule in terms of a Jacobian matrix. Let us recall the Jacobian matrix  $J_{t}(x) = \frac{\partial g_{t}(x)}{\partial x}$  defined in (37). In view of the expression (38) as well as (6) (i.e.,  $X_{t} - \sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}} X_{0} = \sqrt{1 - \overline{\alpha}_{t}}\overline{W}_{t}$  with  $\overline{W}_t\sim \mathcal{N}(0,I_d)$ ), one can write

$$
\begin{array}{l} J _ {t} (x) = I _ {d} + \mathbb {E} \left[ \overline {{W}} _ {t} \mid X _ {t} = x \right] \left(\mathbb {E} \left[ \overline {{W}} _ {t} \mid X _ {t} = x \right]\right) ^ {\top} - \mathbb {E} \left[ \overline {{W}} _ {t} \overline {{W}} _ {t} ^ {\top} \mid X _ {t} = x \right] \\ = I _ {d} + \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x) s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x) ^ {\top} - \mathbb {E} \left[ \overline {{W}} _ {t} \overline {{W}} _ {t} ^ {\top} \mid X _ {t} = x \right], \tag {206} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last line makes use of the relation (36). Additionally, recall that (i)  $v_{t}(x,z)$  (cf. (32)) is the MMSE estimator for estimating  $\overline{W}_t\overline{W}_t z$  given  $\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_t} X_0 + \sqrt{1 - \overline{\alpha}_t}\overline{W}_t = x$  and  $Z_{t} = z$ , and (ii)  $Z_{t}$  is independent from  $X_0$  and  $\overline{W}_t$ . Then this MMSE estimator admits the following expression (Hajek, 2015, Section 3.3.1):

$$
v _ {t} (x, z) = \mathbb {E} \left[ \bar {W} _ {t} \bar {W} _ {t} ^ {\top} z \mid X _ {t} = x \right] = \mathbb {E} \left[ \bar {W} _ {t} \bar {W} _ {t} ^ {\top} \mid X _ {t} = x \right] z. \tag {207}
$$

As a result, the mapping introduced in (33b) can be alternatively expressed as:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Psi_ {t} (x, z) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} \Big (x + (1 - \alpha_ {t}) s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x) \Big) + \sigma_ {t} \left\{z - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \left[ z + (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x) s _ {t} ^ {\star} (x) ^ {\top} z - v _ {t} (x, z) \right] \right\} \\ = \mu_ {t} (x) + \sigma_ {t} \left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right)} J _ {t} (x)\right) z, \tag {208} \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have also used the definition (74b) of  $\mu_t(\cdot)$ . In comparison to the plain DDPM-type sampler (cf. (30)), the key correction term is the second component on the right-hand side of (208), which adjusts the covariance of the additive Gaussian noise.

Equipped with the above expression (208), we can readily express the conditional distribution of  $Y_{t - 1}$  (cf. (33a)) given  $Y_{t}$  such that: for any points  $x_{t}, x_{t - 1} \in \mathbb{R}^{d}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {Y _ {t - 1} \mid Y _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) = \frac {1}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {d / 2} \left| \det \left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} J _ {t} (x _ {t})\right) \right|} \\ \cdot \exp \left(- \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} \left\| \left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {- 1} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}\right). \tag {209} \\ \end{array}
$$

Step 2: controlling the conditional distributions  $p_{X_{t-1}|X_t}$  and  $p_{Y_{t-1}|Y_t}$ . Akin to Step 2 in the proof of Theorem 3, we need to look at the conditional distribution  $p_{X_{t-1}|X_t}$  when restricted to points from the following set:

$$
\mathcal {E} := \left\{\left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \mid - \log p _ {X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t}\right) \leq \frac {1}{2} c _ {6} d \log T, \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} \leq c _ {3} \sqrt {d \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \log T} \right\}, \tag {210}
$$

with the numerical constants  $c_{3}, c_{6} > 0$  introduced in Lemma 2. The following lemma, which is a counterpart of Lemma 8 for the accelerated sampler, characterizes  $p_{X_{t-1}|X_t}$  in a fairly tight manner over the set  $\mathcal{E}$ . The proof is deferred to Appendix E.2.

Lemma 12. There exists some large enough numerical constant  $c_{\zeta} > 0$  such that: for every  $(x_{t}, x_{t-1}) \in \mathcal{E}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}) = \frac {1}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {d / 2} \left| \det  \left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} J _ {t} (x _ {t})\right) \right|} \\ \cdot \exp \left(- \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} \left\| \left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t})} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {- 1} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \zeta_ {t} \left(x _ {t - 1}, x _ {t}\right)\right) \tag {211} \\ \end{array}
$$

holds for some residual term  $\zeta_t(x_{t-1}, x_t)$  obeying

$$
\left| \zeta_ {t} \left(x _ {t - 1}, x _ {t}\right) \right| \leq c \zeta \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {4 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}. \tag {212}
$$

Here, we recall the definition of  $\mu_t(\cdot)$  (resp.  $J_t(\cdot)$ ) in (74b) (resp. (37)).

Moving beyond the set  $\mathcal{E}$ , we are still in need of bounding the log density ratio  $\log \frac{p_{X_{t-1}}|X_t}{p_{Y_{t-1}}|Y_t}$  for all pairs  $(x_t, x_{t-1})$  outside  $\mathcal{E}$ , in a way similar to Lemma 9 in the proof of Theorem 3. Our crude bound towards this end is stated as follows, whose proof is postponed to Appendix E.3.

Lemma 13. For all  $(x_{t},x_{t - 1})\in \mathbb{R}^{d}\times \mathbb{R}^{d}$  , we have

$$
\log \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right)}{p _ {Y _ {t - 1} \mid Y _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right)} \leq T ^ {c _ {0} + 2 c _ {R} + 2} \left\{\left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \left\| x _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + 1 \right\}, \tag {213}
$$

where  $c_{0}$  is defined in (22).

Step 3: bounding the KL divergence of interest. With Lemmas 12-13 in place, one can repeat the arguments in Step 3 in the proof of Theorem 3 to arrive at

$$
\mathbb {E} _ {x _ {t} \sim X _ {t}} \left[ \mathrm {K L} \left(p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} (\cdot \mid x _ {t}) \| p _ {Y _ {t - 1} \mid Y _ {t}} (\cdot \mid x _ {t})\right) \right] \lesssim \left(\frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {4 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}\right) ^ {2}.
$$

Substitution into (79) and (78) then yields

$$
2 \mathsf {T V} (p _ {X _ {1}}, p _ {Y _ {1}}) ^ {2} \leq \mathsf {K L} (p _ {X _ {1}} \| p _ {Y _ {1}}) \lesssim \mathsf {K L} (p _ {X _ {T}} \| p _ {Y _ {T}}) + \sum_ {t \geq 2} ^ {T} \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {9} T}{T ^ {3}} \asymp \frac {d ^ {6} \log^ {9} T}{T ^ {2}},
$$

where the last relation results from (122). This completes the proof of Theorem 4.

# E.2 Proof of Lemma 12

Recall that an explicit expression for  $p_{X_{t-1} \mid X_t}(x_{t-1} \mid x_t)$  has already been established in Lemma 8 (see (160)). A little algebra then allows one to write

$$
\left. p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) = f _ {1} \left(x _ {t}\right) \exp \left(- f _ {2} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) + \zeta_ {t, 1} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right)\right), \right. \tag {214}
$$

for some function  $f_{1}(\cdot)$ , where

$$
g _ {t - 1} (x) := \int_ {x _ {0}} \left(x - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) p _ {X _ {0} | X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0}, \tag {215a}
$$

$$
f _ {2} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) = \frac {\left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} x _ {t - 1} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} + \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} + \frac {\frac {1}{2} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} J _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}, \tag {215b}
$$

$$
\zeta_ {t, 1} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) = \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {1} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \gamma \left[ J _ {t - 1} \left((1 - \tau) \widehat {x} _ {t} + \tau x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) - J _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \right] \mathrm {d} \tau \mathrm {d} \gamma}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right), \tag {215c}
$$

and we remind the readers that  $x_{t}(\gamma) = \gamma x_{t - 1} + (1 - \gamma)\widehat{x}_{t}$  with  $\widehat{x}_t = x_t / \sqrt{\alpha_t}$ .

In order to control (214), we single out two useful facts: for every  $(x_{t},x_{t - 1})\in \mathcal{E}$  (cf. (210)),

$$
\left\| J _ {t - 1} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) - J _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \right\| \lesssim d ^ {2} \sqrt {\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}} \log^ {2} T, \quad \forall \gamma \in [ 0, 1 ] \tag {216}
$$

and

$$
\left\| \frac {J _ {t - 1} (\widehat {x} _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {J _ {t} (x _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right\| \lesssim \frac {d ^ {2} (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log^ {2} T}{(\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}}. \tag {217}
$$

The proofs of these two facts are postponed to Appendix E.2.1. These facts in turn allow us to bound

$$
\left| \zeta_ {t, 1} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \right| \leq \frac {\left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}\right)} \sup  _ {\gamma \in [ 0, 1 ]} \left\| J _ {t - 1} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) - J _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \right\| \lesssim d ^ {3} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \log^ {3} T \tag {218}
$$

and

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} J _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right| \\ \leq \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \left\| \frac {J _ {t - 1} (\widehat {x} _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {J _ {t} (x _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right\| \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3} (1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2} \log^ {3} T}{(\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}} \asymp \frac {d ^ {3} (1 - \alpha_ {t}) ^ {2} \log^ {3} T}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) ^ {2}} \tag {219} \\ \end{array}
$$

for any  $(x_{t},x_{t - 1})\in \mathcal{E}$ . Consequently, there exists some function  $f_{3}(\cdot)$  such that

$$
\left. p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) = f _ {3} \left(x _ {t}\right) \exp \left(- f _ {4} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) + \zeta_ {t, 2} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right)\right), \right. \tag {220a}
$$

where

$$
f _ {4} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) = \frac {\alpha_ {t} \| \widehat {x} _ {t} - x _ {t - 1} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} + \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} + \frac {\frac {1}{2} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}, \tag {220b}
$$

$$
\left| \zeta_ {t, 2} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \right| \lesssim d ^ {3} \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \log^ {3} T \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {4 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}. \tag {220c}
$$

To continue, we further observe that

$$
\left| \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right| \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {3 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}, \tag {221}
$$

which is an immediate consequence of the following two bounds (obtained using (161c), (161b) and (39b)):

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} g _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right| \lesssim d ^ {2} \sqrt {\frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) ^ {3}}{\left(\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right) ^ {3}}} \log^ {2} T \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {3 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}} \\ \left| \left(1 - \sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}\right) \frac {(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}) ^ {\top} g _ {t} (x _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right| \lesssim \frac {d \log^ {2} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

This bound (221) allows us to replace the second term on the right-hand side of (220b) with  $\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_t} (x_{t-1} - \widehat{x}_t)^\top g_t(x_t)}{1 - \overline{\alpha}_t}$ . It is also seen from (161a) and the properties (39) that

$$
\left\| \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right)} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| \lesssim \frac {\log T}{T} \cdot d \log T \asymp \frac {d \log^ {2} T}{T} = o (1), \tag {222}
$$

and therefore,

$$
\left\| (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \frac {(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}) ^ {\top} J _ {t} (x _ {t}) (x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t})}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \right\| \lesssim \frac {d \log^ {2} T}{T} \cdot \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} \lesssim \frac {(1 - \alpha_ {t}) d ^ {2} \log^ {3} T}{T} \lesssim \frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T ^ {2}}.
$$

These combined with (220) allow us to show that: there exist some functions  $f_{5}(\cdot)$  and  $\widetilde{f}_{5}(\cdot)$  such that

$$
\left. p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) = f _ {5} \left(x _ {t}\right) \exp \left(- f _ {6} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) + \zeta_ {t, 3} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right)\right) \right. \tag {223a}
$$

where

$$
\begin{array}{l} f _ {6} (x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}) = \frac {\alpha_ {t} \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} + \frac {\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}) ^ {\top} g _ {t} (x _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} + \frac {\frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {t} (x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}) ^ {\top} J _ {t} (x _ {t}) (x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \\ = \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} \left\{\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) ^ {\top} J _ {t} (x _ {t}) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \right\} + \widetilde {f} _ {t} (x _ {t}), \quad (2 2 3 \mathrm {b}) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\left| \zeta_ {t, 3} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \right| \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {4 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}. \tag {223c}
$$

To further proceed, we make note of another useful fact:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{1 - \alpha_ {t}} \left| (x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t}) \left(\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} J _ {t} (x _ {t})\right) \left(\frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} g _ {t} (x _ {t})\right) \right. \right| \\ \leq \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{1 - \alpha_ {t}} \big \| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \big \| _ {2} \cdot \bigg \| \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} J _ {t} (x _ {t}) \bigg \| \cdot \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \mathbb {E} \left[ \big \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \big \| _ {2} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \\ \lesssim \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T} \cdot \frac {d \log^ {2} T}{T} \cdot \frac {\log T}{T} \cdot \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) \log T} \\ \succ \frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T ^ {2}} \sqrt {1 - \alpha_ {t}} \lesssim \frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have made use of the crude bound (222) in conjunction with the properties (39). Taking this observation together with (223) and (74b), we can apply a little algebra to derive

$$
\left. p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) = f _ {7} \left(x _ {t}\right) \exp \left(- f _ {8} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) + \zeta_ {t, 4} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right)\right) \right. \tag {224a}
$$

for some function  $f_7(\cdot)$ , where

$$
f _ {8} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) = \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left\{\left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {\top} \left(I + \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right)} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) \right\}, \tag {224b}
$$

$$
\left| \zeta_ {t, 4} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \right| \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {4 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}. \tag {224c}
$$

Note, however, that the covariance matrix  $I + \frac{1 - \alpha_t}{2(1 - \overline{\alpha}_t)} J_t(x_t)$  still differs from the desired one ( $I - \frac{1 - \alpha_t}{2(1 - \overline{\alpha}_t)} J_t(x_t)$ ) $^{-2}$ ). As it turns out, these two matrices are fairly close to each other. To see this, we write

$$
\left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} J _ {t} (x _ {t})\right) ^ {- 2} = I + \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} J _ {t} (x _ {t}) + A,
$$

where  $A$  is a matrix obeying (see (222))

$$
\left\| A \right\| \lesssim \left\| \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right)} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| ^ {2} \lesssim \frac {d ^ {2} \log^ {4} T}{T ^ {2}}.
$$

Consequently, we can demonstrate that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} \left\| \left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} J _ {t} (x _ {t})\right) ^ {- 1} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t})\right) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \\ = \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} \left\{\left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t})\right) ^ {\top} \left(I + \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} J _ {t} (x _ {t})\right) \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t})\right) \right\} + O \left(\frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} \| A \| \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \alpha_ {t})} \big (x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t}) \big) ^ {\top} \Bigg (I + \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} J _ {t} (x _ {t}) \Bigg) \big (x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t}) \big) + O \Bigg (\frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {5} T}{T ^ {2}} \Bigg).
$$

To see why the last line holds, note that (according to Lemma 1 and the properties (39))

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} \leq \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} + \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}} (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \| \mathbb {E} \left[ \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0} \| _ {2} \mid X _ {t} = x _ {t} \right] \| _ {2} \\ \lesssim \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T} + \sqrt {\frac {d \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}} (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \asymp \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}, \\ \end{array}
$$

and hence

$$
\frac {\alpha_ {t}}{1 - \alpha_ {t}} \| A \| \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t}) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {5} T}{T ^ {2}}.
$$

Combining the above bound with (224), we arrive at

$$
\left. p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t}\right) = f _ {9} \left(x _ {t}\right) \exp \left(- f _ {1 0} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) + \zeta_ {t, 5} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right)\right) \right. \tag {225a}
$$

for some function  $f_{9}(\cdot)$ , where

$$
f _ {1 0} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) = \frac {\alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)} \left\| \left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}\right)} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {- 1} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}, \tag {225b}
$$

$$
\left| \zeta_ {t, 5} \left(x _ {t}, x _ {t - 1}\right) \right| \lesssim \frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {4 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}. \tag {225c}
$$

To finish up, repeat Step 3 in the proof of Lemma 8 to yield

$$
f _ {7} \left(x _ {t}\right) = \left(1 + O \left(\frac {d ^ {3} \log^ {4 . 5} T}{T ^ {3 / 2}}\right)\right) \frac {1}{\left(2 \pi \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}}\right) ^ {d / 2} | \det  \left(I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) |}
$$

as claimed. This combined with (225) concludes the proof.

# E.2.1 Proof of auxiliary claims in Lemma 12

Proof of relation (216). For any  $(x_{t},x_{t - 1})\in \mathcal{E}$ , one necessarily has

$$
\left\| x _ {t} (\gamma) - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} \leq \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} \leq c _ {3} \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}. \tag {226}
$$

Given  $x_{t}$ , we define the set

$$
\mathcal {E} _ {1} := \left\{x: \| \widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x \| _ {2} \leq c _ {4} \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) \log T} \right\}.
$$

Then for any  $x_0\in \mathcal{E}_1$  , one has

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \leq \max  \left\{\| \widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \| _ {2}, \| \widehat {x} _ {t} - x _ {t - 1} \| _ {2} \right\} \leq \max  \left\{c _ {4} \sqrt {d (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) \log T}, c _ {3} \sqrt {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T} \right\} \\ = c _ {4} \sqrt {d (1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}) \log T}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality comes from (39b). This in turn reveals that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (x _ {t} (\gamma) \mid x _ {0})}{p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (\widehat {x} _ {t} \mid x _ {0})} - 1 \right| = \left| \exp \left(\frac {\left\| \widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})} - \frac {\left\| x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})}\right) - 1 \right| \\ \leq \left| \exp \left(\frac {\left\| \widehat {x} _ {t} - x _ {t} (\gamma) \right\| _ {2} \left\{\left\| \widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} + \left\| x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \right\}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})}\right) - 1 \right| \\ \lesssim \frac {\left\| \widehat {x} _ {t} - x _ {t} (\gamma) \right\| _ {2} \left\{\left\| \widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} + \left\| x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \right\}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1})} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\lesssim d \sqrt {\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}} \log T \lesssim d \sqrt {\frac {\log^ {3} T}{T}} = o (1),
$$

where the second line follows from the elementary relation

$$
\left| \| a \| _ {2} ^ {2} - \| b \| _ {2} ^ {2} \right| = \left| \| a \| _ {2} - \| b \| _ {2} \right| \cdot \left(\| a \| _ {2} + \| b \| _ {2}\right) \leq \| a - b \| _ {2} \left(\| a \| _ {2} + \| b \| _ {2}\right),
$$

and the last line relies on (39) and our assumption on  $T$ . Moreover, repeating the same argument as in (116) and (120), we arrive at

$$
\frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t} (\gamma))}{p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (\widehat {x} _ {t})} = 1 + O \left(d \sqrt {\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}} \log T\right). \tag {227}
$$

Putting the above results together leads to

$$
\frac {p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t} (\gamma))}{p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t})} = \frac {p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (x _ {t} (\gamma) \mid x _ {0}) / p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {t} (\gamma))}{p _ {X _ {t - 1} \mid X _ {0}} (\widehat {x} _ {t} \mid x _ {0}) / p _ {X _ {t - 1}} (\widehat {x} _ {t})} = 1 + O \left(d \sqrt {\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}} \log T\right).
$$

Equipped with the above relation, we can demonstrate that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t} (\gamma)) (x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}) (\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \\ \leq O \left(d \sqrt {\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}} \log T\right) \cdot \left(\int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) \left\| x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} d x _ {0}\right) \\ + \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) - \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} (228) \\ \lesssim \sqrt {d ^ {3} \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \log^ {3} T}, (229) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last step invokes the property (43a). Following similar arguments (which we omit for brevity), we can also derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x _ {t} (\gamma) - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right. \\ \left. - \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} (x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}) \left(\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) \left(\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| \\ \lesssim d ^ {2} \sqrt {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) \left(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}\right)} \log^ {2} T, \tag {230} \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have made use of the property (43b). Taking the above two above perturbation bounds together with the expression (94) and making use of (43a) immediately lead to the advertised result:

$$
\left\| J _ {t - 1} \left(x _ {t} (\gamma)\right) - J _ {t - 1} \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \right\| \lesssim d ^ {2} \sqrt {\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}} \log^ {2} T. \tag {231}
$$

Proof of relation (217). To establish this relation, we first apply the triangle inequality:

$$
\left\| \frac {J _ {t - 1} (\widehat {x} _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {J _ {t} (x _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \right\| \leq \left\| \frac {J _ {t - 1} (\widehat {x} _ {t}) - J _ {t} (x _ {t})}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \right\| + \left\| \left(\frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}}\right) J _ {t} (x _ {t}) \right\|.
$$

Let us first consider the second term

$$
\left\| \left(\frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}} - \frac {1}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \right\| = \frac {\bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1} \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right)}{\left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}\right) \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right)} \| J _ {t} \left(x _ {t}\right) \| \leq \frac {\bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1} \left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) d \log T}{\left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t - 1}\right) \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right)} \lesssim \frac {\left(1 - \alpha_ {t}\right) d \log T}{\left(\alpha_ {t} - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right) ^ {2}}. \tag {232}
$$

where the last inequality uses (161a) and the properties (39).

Next, we move on to bound the difference  $J_{t - 1}(\widehat{x}_t) - J_t(x_t)$ . By virtue of the relation (173), one can deduce that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| \\ \leq \frac {1}{\sqrt {\alpha_ {t}}} O \left(\frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}}\right) \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} d x _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

In addition, recognizing that  $\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_t}} = 1 + \frac{1 - \alpha_t}{\sqrt{\alpha_t} (1 + \sqrt{\alpha_t})} = 1 + O(1 - \alpha_t)$ , we can further invoke the triangle inequality and (39a) to obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \\ \lesssim \frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} (x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}) \big \| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0} \big \| _ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Repeating the same argument also reveals that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t - 1}} \left(x _ {0} \mid \widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \left(\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) \left(\widehat {x} _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} - \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| \\ \lesssim \frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\|. \\ \end{array}
$$

In view of the expression (94) for  $J_{t}$ , combining the preceding two bounds with a little algebra yields

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \left\| J _ {t - 1} (\widehat {x} _ {t}) - J _ {t} (x _ {t}) \right\| \\ \lesssim \frac {1}{(1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}) (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} \frac {d (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log T}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t - 1}} \\ \cdot \left\{\left\| \int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right) \left(x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mathrm {d} x _ {0} \right\| + \left(\int p _ {X _ {0} \mid X _ {t}} \left(x _ {0} \mid x _ {t}\right) \left\| x _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} x _ {0} \right\| _ {2} \mathrm {d} x _ {0}\right) ^ {2} \right\} \\ \lesssim \frac {d ^ {2} (1 - \alpha_ {t}) \log^ {2} T}{(\alpha_ {t} - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}) ^ {2}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last line follows from Lemma 1 and the properties (39).

Putting the above bounds together immediately establishes relation (217).

# E.3 Proof of Lemma 13

According to the expression (209), one has

$$
Y _ {t - 1} \mid Y _ {t} = x _ {t} \sim \mathcal {N} \bigg (\mu_ {t} (x _ {t}), \underbrace {\frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}} \bigg (I - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 (1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t})} J _ {t} (x _ {t}) \bigg) ^ {2}} _ {=: \Sigma (\widehat {x} _ {t})} \bigg).
$$

In order to quantify the density, we first bound the Jacobian matrix  $J_{t}(x)$  defined in (37). On the one hand, the expression (38) tells us that  $J_{t}(x) \preceq I_{d}$  for any  $x$ , given that the term within the curly bracket in (38) is a negative covariance matrix. On the other hand,  $J_{t}(x)$  can be lower bounded by

$$
J _ {t} (x) \succeq - \frac {1}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left(X _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0}\right) \left(X _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} X _ {0}\right) ^ {\top} \mid X _ {t} = x \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \succeq - \frac {\mathbb {E} \left[ \left\| X _ {t} - \sqrt {\overline {{\alpha_ {t}}}} X _ {0} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \mid X _ {t} = x \right]}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} I _ {d} \succeq - \frac {2 \| x \| _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 T ^ {2 c _ {R}}}{1 - \overline {{\alpha}} _ {t}} I _ {d} \\ \succeq - T ^ {c _ {0} + 1} \left(\| x \| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}}\right) I _ {d}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second line applies the assumption that  $\| X_0\| _2\leq T^{c_R}$ , and the last line invokes the choice (22). As a consequence, we have

$$
\Sigma \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \succeq \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}} \left(1 - \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{2 \left(1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}\right)}\right) ^ {2} I _ {d} = \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{4 \alpha_ {t}} \left(\frac {1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t} + \alpha_ {t} - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}{1 - \bar {\alpha} _ {t}}\right) ^ {2} I _ {d} \succeq \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{4 \alpha_ {t}} I _ {d} \succeq \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{4} I _ {d}; \tag {233a}
$$

$$
\Sigma \left(\widehat {x} _ {t}\right) \preceq \frac {1 - \alpha_ {t}}{\alpha_ {t}} T ^ {2 c _ {0} + 2} \left(2 \| \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {4} + 2 T ^ {4 c _ {R}}\right) I _ {d} \preceq 4 T ^ {2 c _ {0} + 2} \left(\| \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {4} + T ^ {4 c _ {R}}\right) I _ {d}. \tag {233b}
$$

With the above relations in mind, we are ready to bound the density function  $p_{Y_{t-1} \mid Y_t}(x_{t-1} \mid x_t)$  for any  $x_t, x_{t-1} \in \mathbb{R}^d$ . It is seen from (209) that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log \frac {1}{p _ {Y _ {t - 1} | Y _ {t}} (x _ {t - 1} \mid x _ {t})} = \frac {\left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t})\right) ^ {\top} \left(\Sigma (\widehat {x} _ {t})\right) ^ {- 1} \left(x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t})\right)}{2} + \frac {1}{2} \log \det  \left(\Sigma (\widehat {x} _ {t})\right) + \frac {d}{2} \log (2 \pi) \\ \leq \frac {2 \| x _ {t - 1} - \mu_ {t} (x _ {t}) \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{1 - \alpha_ {t}} + \frac {d}{2} \log \left(8 \pi T ^ {2 c _ {0} + 2} \left(\| \widehat {x} _ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {4} + T ^ {4 c _ {R}}\right)\right) \\ \leq 2 T ^ {c _ {0} + 1} \left\{2 \left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \left\| x _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + T ^ {2 c _ {R}} \right\} + \frac {d}{2} \log \left(8 \pi T ^ {2 c _ {0} + 2} \left(\left\| \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {4} + T ^ {4 c _ {R}}\right)\right) \\ \leq T ^ {c _ {0} + 2 c _ {R} + 2} \left\{\left\| x _ {t - 1} - \widehat {x} _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \left\| x _ {t} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + 1 \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second inequality results from (233), and the third inequality makes use of (177) and (22). Given that  $\log \frac{p_{X_{t - 1}|X_t}(x_{t - 1} | x_t)}{p_{Y_{t - 1}|Y_t}(x_{t - 1} | x_t)} \leq \log \frac{1}{p_{Y_{t - 1}|Y_t}(x_{t - 1} | x_t)}$ , we have concluded the proof.
